## El Salvador

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. On this auspicious occasion of your appearance in this world, we are moved to reflect within our minds on the wonderful gift you have given us in the form of this wonderful Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.

This wonderful Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is not the fruit of any mundane person. No mundane leader, regardless of his academic ability, wealth, managerial prowess, or even his ability to lecture on *Bhāgavata* philosophy, could have established this movement. This movement is cent percent spiritual, an extension of the eternal pastimes of the Lord. It has appeared in this world not by mundane arrangement but by your mercy as the topmost pure devotee of the Lord. Just as Lord Kṛṣṇa appeared as the son of His pure devotees Vasudeva and Devakī, so the Lord has happily allowed you to serve Him as the founder of this movement.

As the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is an incarnation of the Lord, so this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is also an incarnation of the Lord. The proof is that, just as a sincere devotee can achieve all perfection of life by following the teachings of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, so he can also achieve all perfection by properly associating with the devotees in this movement.

The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* teaches us of the Lord’s multi-incarnations so that we may become attracted to His transcendental pastimes and aspire to go back to Godhead. Similarly, by dedicating our lives to this movement, we can experience the wonderful adventure of becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* describes the great trials and tribulations of the Lord’s devotees, and their ultimate success by surrendering to Him. Similarly, the devotees in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement constantly face opposition from the demons, and even from within our own minds, but by taking shelter of Lord Kṛṣṇa we directly experience how He changes the greatest apparent defeat into the greatest victory.

Although a person may have been destined to suffer or enjoy a certain amount of *karma,* when he takes shelter of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, everything is adjusted to help him advance in spiritual life. Similarly, by the influence of this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, all world affairs are being automatically adjusted to ultimately lead everyone to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We have seen that parts of the world, like Eastern Europe, that were at one time practically closed to religion are now the greatest preaching fields in the world. This is not the result of any mundane social or political movement. It has happened by the influence of this great Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. The fact is that all political, economic, and social conditions of the world are being imperceptibly influenced by this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.

We have seen that although most people in the world have little interest in God, so much opulence comes to your movement just by our selling your books and chanting the Lord’s holy names. The *karmīs* are amazed that although the devotees do not work hard like they do, they are surrounded by opulence and are happy. They cannot understand this great fortune of the devotees because they are seeing this movement as a mundane organization. But factually, as the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* describes the great opulence of Vṛndāvana and other societies of devotees and kingdoms ruled by *rājarṣis*, so that same opulence is gradually coming to this movement by the grace of the Lord. Without this great Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, none of this would be possible. The world would be totally under the control of Kali.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us a wonderful gift in the form of this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. We can especially appreciate your greatness and purity when we see the transcendental nature of the movement that you established. If we simply carry out your instructions and work cooperatively to spread this movement, we will relish excitement and bliss as we watch this movement expand by the Lord’s mercy. You have given us a golden opportunity to live very happy, exciting lives as we purify ourselves in preparation for going back home, back to Godhead. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble servants in El Salvador.

## Fiji

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

All glories to your ISKCON society, which is doing the highest welfare work by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we have neither the words nor the ability to describe your unlimited glorious pastimes. Please excuse this inadequate attempt of ours.

We often meditate on how, during the final days of your earthly pastimes, you expressed great concern over your FIji *yātrā,* which was then in its formative stages, the temple construction having just begun. In those days your health was very fragile, and all your disciples thought you would leave this material world at any moment. Yet in that condition only a transcendental personality of your caliber could show such a fatherly concern over his youngest child.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in Lautoka your Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Kāliya Temple, which had been damaged by some demoniac arsonists in late 1989, is now fully repaired and has been functioning normally since July of last year. By your mercy we were able to assist in the reconstruction work and improve on some of the facilities.

We pray to you humbly for your able protection of the Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Kāliya Temple, just as you did previously.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once told Vāsudeva Prabhu that Fiji would become Kṛṣṇa conscious through distribution of *prasādam*. Lord Kṛṣṇa is fulfilling His pure devotee’s desire, as we now have five successful restaurants operating in your Fiji *yātrā*.

Śrīla Vyāsadeva has stated that people who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the Age of Kali shall get light from the *Bhāgavata Purāṇa: kalau naṣṭa-dṛśām eṣa purāṇārko ’dhunoditaḥ*.

Being a bona fide representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, you have kindly translated the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* into English and explained it with your invaluable Bhaktivedanta purports, and have further empowered your disciples to translate your books into many languages. Now people all over the world are discovering for the first time who God is, what His name is, where He stays, what He eats, what He does, who His friends are, and many other details about Him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, only you could give the whole world all the above intimate and confidential information, as you are a personal friend of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, some years ago a *sannyāsī* from South India belonging to the Śrī-sampradāya visited the Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Kāliya temple for a week or so. During his stay he narrated a wonderful pastime of yours. Once he was on his way to the United States on a preaching tour when he met you in Bombay at your Bombay temple. He told us that he had known you for some years and was a personal friend of yours. When he told you that he was on his way to the U.S., you asked him where he intended to stay there. He replied that he didn’t have any firm arrangements. You kindly offered that he could stay at ISKCON centers, and you gave him a letter of introduction addressed to all temple presidents stating that this Vaiṣṇava should be accommodated and given the same respect and treatment they would extend to you. The *sannyāsī* spent a few months in the U.S., and on returning to Bombay he visited your temple again. On seeing you there he immediately offered you *daṇḍavat praṇāmas,* catching hold of your lotus feet. Thereafter, when you asked him how his stay in the U.S. had been, he replied, “You have great disciples! They took wonderful care of me.” On hearing this, tears of love welled in your lotus eyes and rolled down your cheeks.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have always said that the success of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement was due to your spiritual master, who very kindly sent you assistance in the form of your great disciples.

In this material world, all living entities from the ant to the demigods claim the biggest credit for the smallest act they perform. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, never took even the smallest credit for the greatest feat ever achieved.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the depth of your humility and love for your disciples is unfathomable.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said, “I am a servant of God; God will give me protection. Why should I worry for anything?”

Who but a great *mahājana* like yourself could say this with such conviction? On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we humbly pray for your mercy so that we may endeavor to follow in the footsteps of your faithful and glorious servants.

Your servants and the servants of your servants in Fiji.

## Florianopolis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

The purpose of this Vyāsa-pūjā offering is to celebrate your transcendental appearance on this planet, which for us is a cause of great jubilation and joy. You have saved us from falling into the dark well of material existence and elevated us to the highest position, which is devotional service unto the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to be your servants, your instruments. Allow us to have this relationship with you. Please don’t kick us away. Let us be your book distributors. We know that you want us to distribute these books. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, send us nice people who like to read your books, who want to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. Allow us to be useful at your glorious lotus feet.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us to become unconditional servants of your servants. Please help us to make Brazil and the whole planet Kṛṣṇa conscious. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to help you in your mission of saving the whole world from chaos, anguish, and despair. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us to be humble, tolerant, and compassionate with everyone.

You are the direct representative of Lord Caitanya and Lord Kṛṣṇa. Please be tolerant with us, accept our prayers, and give us your blessings. The grandfather is the well-wisher of his beloved grandchildren, who pray so much for his mercy.

Here in Florianopolis we are dancing, singing, taking *prasādam,* following all the spiritual programs, following the four regulative principles, chanting sixteen rounds a day, reading *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā* regularly, worshiping Tulasī-devī, distributing *prasādam* on the streets, and mainly distributing books.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, I hope you become satisfied with this humble attempt of your servants from Florianopolis to worship and serve you.

Thank you very much for the unlimited kindness you have shown in saving us and purifying us of material contamination.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!  
Florianopolis Mandira-*kī jaya*!  
*Gaura-premānanda haribol!*

Your attentive servants in Florianopolis, Brazil,

Keśava Swami / Aṣṭa-varga dāsa / Jaya Saṅkīrtana dāsa / Bhakta Mauricio / Bhakta Roberto

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

This year is the twentieth anniversary of your divine appearance in “this remote corner of the world,” and as your dear disciple Hridayānanda dāsa Goswami has so nicely pointed out, “Nothing in the past that has happened in Gainesville nor anything in the future that will happen here is as significant for this town as your appearance here.”

We pray that by holding a big celebration of the anniversary of your appearance here that we and the residents of Gainesville will realize more and more the significant gifts you have given the world and will thus increase our output of devotional service to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Please bless us with the spiritual strength to continue your missionary work in this area or wherever we may go in the future.

Your aspiring servants in Gainesville, Florida,

Dāru Brahma dāsa / Govinda-parāyaṇa dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-śravaṇa dāsa / Bhāva-sindhu dāsa / Bhakta George / Bhakta Chaim / Śrī Kṛṣṇa-prasāda-devī dāsī / Yadu-priyā-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-stuti-devī dāsī

## Gītā-nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, We’re Ready

We fall at your feet,  
You lift us up  
On the end of your cane,  
Where we crawl like a caterpillar.

“Are you ready yet?”  
Your voice is stern but kind.  
“To make the Society more perfect—  
That is why I was coming back  
To Gītā-nāgarī.  
Do you remember?”

We inch up the cane.  
“Fifty percent of my mission  
Remains incomplete.”  
We reach your fingers  
On the wooden hook.  
You won’t let us off.  
“We must organize varṇāśrama—  
Steppingstone for spiritual  
understanding—  
Do you remember?”

We remember, Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
We are a caterpillar, but we  
remember.  
We are a caterpillar becoming  
a butterfly,  
A temple becoming a society,  
And we fall at your feet for help,  
Because by your divine grace  
We’re ready.

Your servants at the Gītā-nāgarī farm community, Port Royal, Pennsylvania.  
(written by Sureśvara Dāsa)

## Greece

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this most auspicious day of your appearance.

Death is the last thing we think about when we are alive. We become so puffed up, even if we are only a little bit materially established. We absorb our minds in objects that are not at all eternal—mostly they endure maybe a little bit longer than we live. Even so, we are very attentive to see that when we die our descendants will be able to keep them. But these objects, sons, wives, and daughters are also temporary; this, by your grace, has become known to us.

Now people say that they can manage their properties and their happiness just like their computers.

This is all illusion, of course. But we would never know this if it weren’t for your words, your message about the supreme God, Kṛṣṇa—if you hadn’t made the supreme sacrifice to bring Kṛṣṇa to West.

Kali-yuga is so heavy, yet you have made our lives so light by giving us consciousness of the soul and of Kṛṣṇa. Now, unfortunately, we use this as the basis for reconstructing our material lives.

Just as the *guru* is one, so real Kṛṣṇa consciousness is unique. Now many things are being said in various circles—”Śrīla Prabhupāda said this,” “Śrīla Prabhupāda said that.” Help us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to understand your true message. Everything is right in front of us, but it is so difficult to understand. Some seem to have understood, and they are very loudly proclaiming it. But experience has taught us that good fortune is on the side of the meek. Those who have given up their own voice and repeated your words have helped us, while others abandoned us.

Your aspiring servants in Greece,  
(written by Prema-bhakti-devī dāsī)

Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Nanda-gopa dāsa

## Guatemala

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet, which can sanctify the entire universe.

Although our ability to glorify you is practically nil, we wish to attempt it again on this transcendental anniversary of your glorious and merciful appearance on this planet.

With the passing of years we are beginning to understand the importance of the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the significance of your movement. Here in Guatemala your books are very well known and appreciated. The seed of your message is fructifying in the hearts of hundreds of people who respect and worship you and are eager for your blessings.

Your unending compassion keeps us continually engaged in devotional service. Now we want to reciprocate and show our gratitude by preaching.

As you explain in your purport to Śr*īmad-Bhāgavatam* 2.1.13: “The third energy, which represents nescience, can bewilder the entire animated universe, but still it is not strong enough to stand in the presence of the Supreme Lord. Nescience stands behind the Personality of Godhead, where she is strong enough to cheat the living beings, and the main symptom of such bewildered people is that they talk nonsense....However, those who are in the presence of the Lord and surrender unto Him cannot be influenced by the cheating energy.”

Your Bhaktivedanta purports can change the destiny of all humanity just as they have changed ours.

By listening to your classes (now translated into Spanish), we receive the inspiration and energy to strengthen our faith.

In conventional education no one is trained to control the senses according to perfect knowledge. Your contribution in reinstating Vedic culture in India and introducing it in the West is not only of unimaginable value, but it is also unique and sublime. Please allow us to be your instruments in this wonderful and transcendental enterprise, although we surely do not deserve such a high privilege.

Please forgive our offenses. Bowing down at your lotus feet, we pray to be able to serve you eternally in love and devotion.

Your servants in Guatemala,

Yamunā-jīvana dāsa / Giridhārī dāsa / Śyāmasundara dāsa / Dāmodara dāsa / Janārdana dāsa / Bhakta Celim / Bhaktin Iris

Congregation

Aniruddha dāsa / Akrūra dāsa / Rūpa-mañjārī dāsa / Urukrama dāsa / Mahā-Viṣṇu dāsa / Kaśyapa dāsa / Ānanda dāsa / Subhadrā-devī dāsī / Virocanā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Rosario

## Hare Krishna Land

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The following poems are an expression of the realization of my helplessness and dependence on the purifying effect of your mercy and association. Please accept these as a humble offering from a very fallen soul whom you have miraculously saved from the depths of ignorance

**Without Your Mercy**

Without your mercy I have no confidence.  
Without your mercy I have no tolerance.  
Without your mercy I am envious.  
Without your mercy I’ll never be serious.

Without your mercy I cannot chant.  
Without your mercy I’m obstinate.  
Without your mercy I have no brain.  
Without your mercy I’d go insane.

Without your mercy I’m unsteady.  
Without your mercy I’m also lazy.  
Without your mercy I can’t be humble.  
Without your mercy I’ll only stumble.

Without your mercy I’m in distress.  
Without your mercy I can’t make progress.  
Without your mercy I have no force.  
Without your mercy I’ll wander off course.

Without your mercy I’m controlled by anger.  
Without your mercy I’m in danger.  
Without your mercy there’s only Māyā’s attraction.  
Without your mercy I’ll join a faction.

Without your mercy I can’t cooperate.  
Without your mercy I procrastinate.  
Without your mercy I hear Māyā call.  
Without your mercy I’ll surely fall.

Without your mercy I neglect your vision.  
Without your mercy I alter your mission.  
Without your mercy I forget your instructions.  
Without your mercy I’ll join Māyā’s production.

By your mercy I’m still going on.  
By your mercy I’m still feeling strong.  
By your mercy I do as you do.  
By your mercy I’ll never forget you.

**Your Lotus Feet**

The lotus feet of Your Divine Grace  
Are the only hope for the human race.  
I bow my head at those lotus feet  
And reside in this sacred retreat.

At your feet my heart is relieved,  
Suffering is finished, and mercy received.  
My only desire is to fill my mind  
With thoughts of your feet divine.

At your feet I find satisfaction.  
May they remain my supreme attraction.  
Your feet possess an unusual power  
To make my heart blossom like a flower.

I fall at your feet and bathe in their dust,  
Confident that this will curb all my lust.  
My only hope is to have a place  
At the feet of Your Divine Grace.

Thus every step I’ll be able to trace  
Back to the Lord’s own secret place.  
Please give me this chance at any cost.  
Without sight of your feet, I’ll be lost.

Your servants at Hare Krishna Land, Mauritius.  
(written by Mahātmā dāsa)

## Heidelberg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Most of us who are living here in Heidelberg temple are your grandchildren who never got to know you personally. There is not really a reason to lament; due to your causeless mercy we are so fortunate to have the chance to be disciples of your sons and representatives. They are following in your footsteps, and they hand on your instructions to us.

But still, whenever we look at pictures and watch videos that present your glorious active temple is located. I heard conch shells blowing so I ventured in and saw a wonderful picture of Śrīla Prabhupāda. I then saw devotees from all over the world dancing and chanting. It was so pleasurable that I soon found myself dancing and chanting with them. They would jump high, and I would try to jump higher.

Then some of the Indian devotees started laughing and shouted, “Hey! Māyāvādī \[impersonalist\] dancing! Māyāvādī dancing! Ha! Ha!Ð The fact that we can’t do it shows our misfortune.

But, nonetheless there is hope:

*tulasī-dala-mātreṇa jalasya culukena vā*  
*vikrīṇīte svam ātmāna bhaktebhyo bhakta-vatsalaḥ*

**“Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is very affectionate toward His devotees, sells Himself to a devotee who offers merely a** ***tulasī*** **leaf and a palmful of water.”**

It is possible to attain your direct association by pleasing you through sacrificial performance: by attentively studying your books, by following the instructions given in your books, by handing on your books to all the conditioned souls, and by preaching, your personal presence can be realized.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please grant us the ability to successfully perform this sacrifice. Please allow us not to become proud like Icarus. Daedalus wanted to leave the island of Crete together with his son Icarus and return to Athens. For this reason he built two pairs of artificial wings from wax and feathers. One pair he tied to his shoulders, the other to the shoulders of his son. Before taking off, Daedalus instructed his son: “Icarus, always stay at medium height, I beg you! If you don’t fly high enough, the ocean waves will dampen your wings and in this way drag you down. And if you rise up too high you will get too close to the sun, and the wax will melt due to the heat. Just always follow me!” Then they both took off into the sky. Quickly they flew above the ocean. But young Icarus, driven by frivolity, did not follow his father’s instructions. He left his father and on his wings went up to higher regions. As soon as the boy got too close to the sun, the wax holding together the feathers of his wings began to melt. The feathers came loose and were blown away by the wind. All of a sudden the boy’s arms were not catching hold anymore, and he fell down into the deep ocean.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are just like the instructing Daedalus, while we are like the inexperienced Icarus. The wings represent devotional service. Flying from Crete to Athens is like returning home to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and the ocean symbolizes the ocean of repeated birth and death. The first danger threatening the wings of devotional service is the waves of the ocean of fruitive activities. The second danger is the destructive heat of the rays of offenses against the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas.

The frivolity of Icarus is our pride and mental speculation. The feathers that came loose from the wings are just like blessings that are lost, and falling into the ocean is like falling back into material existence.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please grant us the ability to follow your flight without deviation. Please care for us like the bird mother who takes the young birds out of the nest to fly for the first time. Please enthuse us to perform book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, *harināma,* and preaching programs just like you would have done.

*yogyatā-vicāre, kichu nāhi pāi*  
*tomāra karuṇā sāra*  
*karuṇā na hoile, kāndiyā kāndiyā,*  
*prāṇa nā rākhibo āra*

**“If you examine me, you will find no qualities. Your mercy is all that I am made of. If you are not merciful unto me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life.”**

Your humble servants in Heidelberg, Germany,  
(written by Hṛdaya Govinda dāsa)

## Helsinki

Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Please accept our most humble obeisances.  
All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
You saved the world! You alone injected  
Kṛṣṇa consciousness  
into the degraded Western countries  
and revived it in India as well.  
It was you who directed  
the flood of love of Godhead  
out of the boundaries of the holy dhāma  
to spread it all over the universe.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories unto you!  
You left Vṛndāvana  
just to give Vṛndāvana to us—  
to the fallen souls of Kali-yuga.  
You are wonderful and your qualities are unique.  
When you start to chant,  
all the people simply stare and become amazed.  
Very expertly you beat the drum,  
both big and small,  
and vibrating Kṛṣṇa’s name,  
you give to our spiritual life the most important key—the key to Kṛṣṇa prema.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya!  
You came to the West, you saw and you won.  
Not only that, but you stayed also.  
And you are still staying. And expanding!  
All over the world is your cooling shadow of mercy  
continuing to spread, giving release  
to the people from the scorching waves of Kali-yuga.  
Hari bol! Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Never was there anyone  
more merciful than you in the past, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Nor is there anyone more merciful now.  
Nor will there be anyone more merciful in the future.  
Raising your hands in the air,  
you simply smile and proclaim,  
“Take it, take it, take it!  
As much Kṛṣṇa prema as you can in this very lifetime! I’ll give it. Free!”  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories unto you!  
Māyāpur dhāma.  
You gave us this wonderful, eternal dhāma.  
“Here,” you said, “here I give you  
the kingdom of God! Now you develop it.  
All of you just worship our Lord Gaurāṅga!  
The Lord of all Lords. So beautiful and sweet.  
And here is Śrī Nitāicandra!  
Simply follow Him, worship Him, and dance with Him. Gaura-premānanda!”  
Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Thank you!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya!  
Who else but you could start this revolution—  
the revolution of book distribution!  
Such a power! Such a force!  
And you’re the general.  
“No problem,” you say, “don’t fear. Just go out.  
And preach! Simply go on and distribute!  
Spread these books all over. Till the day you die—  
just preach; just distribute. And then go back  
to Krsna, back home to our loving Lord!”  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, ki jaya!

All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Finland,

## Holland

“The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on a forest fire to extinguish it, the spiritual master is delivering the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances to the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is an ocean of auspicious qualities.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā day. This day is the most blessed event for all the devotees around the world. Without you where would we be? If you would not have come and brought the solution to all problems, where would we be? Without your transcendental purports in *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* where would we be? Without your determination to come and save us, where would we be? Without your pure vision of how to structure the *saṅkīrtana* movement for the next ten thousand years, where would we be?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no limit to what you have delivered. It’s more than right to state that you are nondifferent from the Absolute Truth. In you there is not a tinge of impurity. Lord Kṛṣṇa has been very kind upon us to allow us to try and become your followers. You have given so much that only one who is very foolish and offensive cannot take shelter of you. We know for sure that without you we would have no chance to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. At most our attempts would end with dreaming in the clouds of the material energy, surrounded by speculators and neophyte transcendentalists. Although there may be many who profess to know the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you are that rare personality who has been able to pick us up out of the dark ignorance of material existence. No ordinary person can perform such an extraordinary task. You are able to save the whole world, and this is your mission and goal: to bring everyone back to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, in the spiritual world.

All these and unlimited other benedictions you have bestowed upon us by teaching us the sublime practice of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, may we one day chant the holy names offenselessly and thus be able to guide all conditioned souls back to your lotus feet. In this way may we show our gratitude and indebtedness to you.

Please allow us to remain in the shelter you have offered us, and please give us the understanding to sincerely engage in your mission in this short lifetime.

Your servants in Holland.  
(written by Brahmarāta dāsa)

## Hong Kong

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

O savior of the fallen, please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

All glories unto you and Lord Gaurāṅga.

When Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva offered Prahlāda Mahārāja benedictions after killing Hiraṇyakaśipu, Prahlāda said, “My dear Lord, O Supreme Personality of Godhead, because I am born in an atheistic family, I am naturally attached to material enjoyment. Therefore, kindly do not tempt me with these illusions. I am very much afraid of material conditions, and I desire to be liberated from materialistic life. It is for this reason that I have taken shelter of Your lotus feet.” Similarly, the Chinese people are always aspiring for material happiness. According to the description of the *Bhāgavatam,* they are anti-Viṣṇu. And we are born in such families. Therefore, to gain something material is our natural propensity. In the same way, we pray that we will not be tempted to enjoy our acquisition but rather use it exclusively in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

We also pray that we may stay tightly together, following the principles of loving exchanges between Vaiṣṇavas, as enunciated by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī in his *Nectar of Devotion* and *Nectar of Instruction,* which you have translated and explained for our benefit. In this way we can attend full temple programs so that we can have sufficient strength to fight *māyā* and carry out your instructions firmly.

Your servants in Hong Kong,

## Honolulu

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a great saint, a giant among men. All that we have of any value, all that we are that is good, is given by you. How fortunate we were and yet how little we realized. As the years pass the realization dawns brighter and we grow more mature in gratitude and appreciation. We owe our lives to you. Your power to transmute lost and distressed seekers into bright, enthusiastic spiritualists was incomparable. **Purity is your force.**

The tools of your trade are *halavā*, *gulābjāmuns,* sweet rice, and simply wonderfuls! Many came to you addicted to the nectar of Love Feast *prasādam*. You fed Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* to the spiritually hungry, and we were satisfied—full up to the neck! You trained us to chant, clean, study, preach, and use everything in Kṛṣṇa’s service. **Utility is your principle.** Let us not waste anything, let us not waste our lives, but stay in the shelter of your lotus feet.

Others of us found a book out there in the ocean of *māyā*. It came to us in many ways. Just as Mother Yaśodā looked into Kṛṣṇa’s mouth and was astonished to see the whole cosmos, so we opened seemingly ordinary books of white paper and black ink to discover the truth of the universe and beyond. Your books are beautiful books, full of picture windows to the spiritual world. **Books are the basis.** Like solid foundation stones, these books are the foundation of Vaiṣṇavism. Let us be the mortar so that the temple of ISKCON can flourish and grow.

Every moment of your life with us was a lesson. Each word was spoken to teach and guide. You pushed us to go out and taste the nectar of preaching. Preaching is life! Preaching is beyond hunger and sleep. Preaching makes us strong and healthy in spiritual life. Preaching was your life. **Preaching is your essence.**

Other things may come and go in the centuries ahead. Temples may fall to ruin. Books may rot with age. War may destroy our lifestyle. But preaching will go on. Your glory will survive the ages. Your message and example will save millions of lost souls.

We thank you again and again, falling down in obeisance to you, our savior.

You come to us in dreams, in waking remembrance, on tapes, in your books, and we go on renewed, refreshed, and hopeful. You walk beside us, you carry us, you call us and beckon us to find Kṛṣṇa, our Lord.

We thank you again and again, falling down in obeisance to you, our spiritual master.

Your servants in Honolulu, Hawaii,

## Houston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvati deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the years since you departed,  
Many have imitated the movement that you started.  
Imitation is itself a well-known form of praise,  
Even though it presents itself in backwards kinds of ways.  
There are those who start a group with a great big fancy name,  
Just to have impressive letterhead and generate some fame.  
But soon the competition starts and politics sets in,  
And all that really matters is what election did they win.  
Some put on thick tilaka but then refuse to bow their head,  
They pretend to worship Kṛṣṇa just to fill their belly and their bed.  
Some might sell some mantras but don’t dare ask them about sin,  
Underneath the holy man disguise their credibility is thin.  
There are those who dress in saffron robes  
And fly jet planes around the globe,  
But while presenting themselves as purists  
They are nothing more than tourists.  
Some prefer to speculate and write books about what they think,  
But that brand of philosophy dries out long before the ink.  
There are those who saw how easy you made it look to start a temple,  
But when they decide to do it they discover it’s not so simple.  
Prabhupāda, you’re humble, but I know how great you are;  
I want a chance to serve you, even though it might be from afar.

Your followers in Houston, Texas.  
(written by Gopālācārya dāsa)

## Johannesburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

We are proud to be your followers. But this pride alone will not fully satisfy us. We need to take up your mission as Your Divine Grace prescribed to us. You said, “Just keep yourself to the standard by regular and attentive chanting, study, preaching, working, *saṅkīrtana,* and distribution of literatures, and *prasādam*. Then everything will be all right.” So when things do go wrong, is it because we have deviated from your simple instruction, or is it because we have concocted our own formula for success?

Whichever the case is, you always stressed these sublime teachings for saving the fallen conditioned souls and for our personal purification. You have executed and delivered the message of your Guru Mahārāja and the *paramparā* without concoction. This was your only credit, you humbly said.

The purity of your movement depends on our following your instructions and not neglecting them. In one letter you wrote, “We are not concerned with any other movement save and except Kṛṣṇa consciousness in its pure form. In India it is said that a little bit of a pure thing is much better than huge volumes of impure, adulterated things.” And again stressing the same point, you wrote, “Our only asset is our purity. *Pavitraṁ paramaṁ bhavān.* You cannot reach Kṛṣṇa without being pure because He is the supreme pure.”

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please grant us implicit faith to follow your instructions strictly. Free us from the evil spells of sense gratification, fruitive work, and mental speculation that cloud our devotion to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Dispel the motives that keep us on the fence of ISKCON. Free us from all offenses, *anarthas,* and ignorance, so that we can taste the nectar of *śuddha-nāma*.

Your servants in Johannesburg, South Africa,  
(written by Tribhaṅga-sundara dāsa)

## Kampala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My Śrīla Prabhupāda, I wanted so much to be near you, but I never could. I wasn’t as fortunate as some of your other disciples who were close to you, who served you in intimate association. But perhaps it’s easier to serve at a distance than to serve close up.

I’m only just discovering—realizing—how special you are. I say how special you are, not were, because you never really left us, did you? I think you’re still guiding us. I don’t understand how, but I think it must be true. I don’t see how we could carry on otherwise. I know I couldn’t carry on if you weren’t guiding me.

I wish I hadn’t wasted so much of my time. I wish I hadn’t been so insincere. I wish, I wish, I wish—I wish so many things, but the reality is I’m just an empty shell, and it is only you who give me life. Without your service I’m just an empty shell, and it is only you give me life. Without your service I’m no longer living.

I’m growing old in your service—I hope I can die in it. At some time my death is going to come, yet my tendency is to fall into illusion at the slightest opportunity and forget the fate that awaits this body. Doctors and nurses and medicines can’t save me—only you can save me, only you can stop the pain.

You gave me only two direct instructions—preach in Africa and organize life membership. How can I say “only”? There are others who would die for an instruction from you. How can I count my blessings? You know me so well and gave me instructions just suited to me. How could you have known? I thought you didn’t know me at all—but of course you do. You know us all.

I know I’ll never understand you, never appreciate you. Sometimes I remember how we used to offer flowers at your feet, praying for your merciful glance. Ah, to hear you sing Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava again! Oh, Prabhupāda, we didn’t know anything about you! I hope that sometime in the future, at some place near yet far away, we’ll all be together again, praying for your merciful glance, offering flowers at your lotus feet.

Your servants in Kampala, Uganda, and on the Francophone Preaching Party,

Jalakāra dāsa / Narahari dāsa / Rājarṣi dāsa / Viṣṇupriyā-devī dāsī / Mukesh Shukla / Partick Muheriwoha

## Kirghizstan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Not long ago the seed of devotional service you had planted in our country, the USSR, sprouted in our republic.

Despite the brief period of time since your visit, a large group of people have already become happy to render homage to the holy name and Your Divine Grace.

We have also realized the all-attractiveness of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, which you have made accessible to everybody on our planet.

Thanks to Your Divine Grace, a place appeared in our town, as well as in other places throughout the world, where one can feel an authentically spiritual atmosphere.

The invaluable knowledge obtained from your books stays deep in our hearts.

With your help, everybody can make contact with Lord Kṛṣṇa.

As it is impossible to understand a foreigner without an interpreter, so it is impossible for us living outside India to understand the message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa without you.

Through your books, all the conditioned souls can understand the advantage of taking up the most elevated activities—devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your books replace all the books of impersonalistic philosophy and other rubbish literature in the houses of those who agree to place them on their bookshelves.

Thanks to you, people of all countries are becoming acquainted with the mightiest name of the Supreme Personality of Godhead—Kṛṣṇa.

Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s divine grace came into the Western countries through you.

You are the via medium through which the whole Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *paramparā* casts its merciful moonlight on everyone, giving everyone a chance to go back to Godhead. Only because of you do any of us have a chance to follow in the footsteps of the great *ācāryas* of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya.*

It is possible to write endlessly about what you have done for the conditioned souls in this cruel world. But even in twenty-four hours we could describe no more than a little drop of the vast ocean of divine qualities you possess, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for you are the topmost pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

We shall always remain indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please keep us strong in devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants in Kirghizstan, USSR.

## Kuala Lumpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our desire that we always remain in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. But we also know that it is very difficult, as we are imperfect beings. But again, we are confident that by your causeless mercy we shall be able to achieve Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

It is our experience here that when we are out on book distribution, we are often questioned, “Who are you? What is your religion?” And we answer, “I am from the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.” And they reply, “Oh, Hare Kṛṣṇa, very nice.” So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, even before we could get to them you have already made them ready to take a book and get the mercy of Lord Caitanya.

And next they ask (pointing to the picture of Your Divine Grace), “Can I meet this man?” Their faces are full of desire to meet you. So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are just trying to connect you with these souls who are very much asking to meet you. I pray that you give us the mercy to carry on with this service.

The people we talk with are of Chinese, Indian, and sometimes even Muslim origin. They all take your books. However, we also pray that Your Divine Grace shall bless us to be expert preachers and to work cooperatively.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we here in Malaysia are very grateful that out of your kind mercy on us you have given us the chance to serve you and your many sincere followers.

Your servants in Malaysia.  
(written by Siṁheśvara dāsa)

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We try to think of you as much as we can, especially on this very special day of your appearance.

To us you embody whatever we could consider success. Nowadays this word “success” is on many people’s minds. Even among our Godbrothers we see so much striving for success—in the temple, the family, the business, and in personal life. Thinking about your life, we can see that you went through it all. We should not think the time of your household life was ordinary, for doing so would be a great mistake. You went through so many struggles—for what? Not for yourself! You did it for us: to showing us what to do and how to do it. You have given us the formula for success, the process, and the example.

The great business and family “gurus” of the nineties tell us that the process of modeling one’s life after that of someone successful is the best and fastest way to success, and we already know that from you. Not only that, but you embodied that model. They tell us to study the lives of great personalities in history, and you gave us that, too. We are advised to find the highest principles and adopt them in our life, making our lives congruent with those principles. You taught us that and even gave us the principles. Then we learn that we must find out what our core desire is and pursue that to become successful. You, however, showed us what the perfection of desire is and how to attain the state of pure, unalloyed desire.

In fact, no matter where we look, all we can see is that you have given us everything. All we are left to do is follow your example. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. By following in your footsteps and accepting the gifts you have given, we are receiving what is unavailable through any other method: pure love of God, the highest understanding of our spiritual identity. By getting a glimpse of what is in store for us, we are starting to understand that this type of success is a causeless gift. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants at the Laguna Beach Temple

## Lansing

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (8.20.9), Bali Mahārāja states to Śukrācārya, “O best of the *brāhmaṇas,* many men have laid down their lives on the battlefield, being unafraid of fighting, but rarely has one gotten the chance to give his accumulated wealth faithfully to a saintly person who creates holy places.”

This is indeed a fact, Śrīla Prabhupāda. In this age, the average person would consider the greatest rarity to be the accumulation of wealth itself. But what a privilege to have such wealth and be able to give it to the perfect person, for the perfect reason, at the perfect time. Such an opportunity is, as you write at the end of the purport, “hardly ever obtained.” And if it is somehow or other obtained, it is usually by a single person or perhaps a select few.

But something wonderful is happening. By your mercy devotees around the world are getting a clue as to the method by which the whole world will be able to obtain this oh-so-rare opportunity. For some reason or other the nondevotees have a penchant for celebrating anniversaries of events, even on a grander scale than the original occurrence. Perhaps this is because of their obsession with “chewing the chewed.” But some of your disciples have started thinking and planning how to engage this propensity of the world at large to involve them in your hundredth Vyāsa-pūjā celebration in 1996. Centennials are especially held to be of great significance, so this will create even more interest. Devotees from all quarters are gearing up to make this the greatest birthday party in the history of mankind.

It would take up too much space on these pages to enumerate all the incredible plans for this occasion. Besides, we don’t want to spoil the surprises. But believe us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we think you will be pleased. In fact, most of the arrangements involve the participation and /or acknowledgement by a great number of living entities on this globe. We are talking about tens of millions of people, or even more. And by honoring your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, they will be giving their time, energy, and attention in the perfect manner, at the perfect time, at the perfect place (your birthplace), to a saintly person (you, Śrīla Prabhupāda), who creates holy places. Who could be more magnanimous and kind than you?

Many times, in preparation for very important and difficult tasks, we hear the term “five-year-plan.” So, finding ourselves five years away from that very special date, we humbly beg and pray that you kindly give us the intelligence to give every citizen on this planet the chance, in one capacity or another, to have the same opportunity that Lord Vāmanadeva gave to Mahājana Bali Mahārāja, because without your mercy they will simply rot in the dry, stale, vomit-eating nightmare called the material world.

All glories to Bali Mahārāja!  
All glories to Lord Vāmanadeva!  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your unworthy servants in Lansing, Michigan,

Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Apūrva dāsa / Kamalinī-devī dāsī

Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Abhay dāsa / Gaura-vāṇī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Dave

Children

Pañca-tattva / Amala-purāṇa

## Leicester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance!

Thank you for giving us our only opportunity to know, love, and serve Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who was a total stranger to us before you came. Our lives amounted to the meaningless pursuit of nothing until, by your limitless mercy, you gave us all a glimpse of the transcendental treasure house of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, a vision of unlimited knowledge and bliss within our true, eternal selves.

May the phoenix of devotional service rise from the ashes of selfishness and apathy within our hearts, so that somehow we may attempt to perform our avowed duty to serve your lotus feet.

Always we have the inspiration of your perfect example and your priceless, eternal association through your divine instructions. Please grant us the blessing that we may remember what you want us to do, so that we may meet every day and every challenge with renewed freshness, in the pioneering spirit with which you built a transcendental house in which all the people of the world can take shelter.

As we struggle to assemble the components of this ever-expanding Kṛṣṇa conscious machine, please guide our hearts so that we can put together with love and devotion an invincible weapon with which to defeat the oppressive energy of *māyā*.

In the heat of this battle, as your movement grows and grows, please help us remember that we are deep within enemy territory and that only by your grace may we find the strength to fight and encourage others to resist the forces of darkness and ignorance that prevail in this atheistic world.

We cannot pretend to be worthy servants, but our failures and shortcomings can always remind us that we need only follow your instructions so that the spark of devotion you ignited in our hearts may be rekindled to burn brightly with the intense desire to serve and surrender at your lotus feet.

Now, as this *yātrā* expands and unites, may we seek your blessing to help us remember that our greater family can grow and stay together only as long as you are in the center, binding us together in real, spiritual love, by the process of *bhakti*, the matchless gift for all mankind, which you bestowed so freely.

Please grant us the ability to spread this movement according to your desire. We aspire to one day show our love for you by serving you properly, in the spirit of brotherhood and unity, for the good of all. Please help us to unite by preaching together on your behalf.

Your wayward children in Leicester, England,

## Lima

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. While writing this offering we have again the opportunity to remember the unlimited mercy that this bereaved planet has received through your Divine Grace.

When the conclusion of Vedic knowledge had almost been lost, even in the land of *dharma,* Bhārata-varṣa, you did what according to material calculations seemed impossible. Just by faithfully following your Guru Mahārāja’s instructions, you have rescued Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s teachings, and through your personal example and pure devotion you have injected *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* into the stone hearts of the Kali-yuga people. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to always take shelter in the shade of your lotus feet. We are such neophytes that sometimes, due to the influence of the illusory energy, we foolishly think that in this material world there is something to enjoy, something we are missing.

On this occasion let us also reaffirm our commitment to follow your instructions, because we know that this is the only way to make spiritual advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Our success in spreading the message of this great movement and in saving the fallen souls depends on this formula. We know that we won’t accomplish anything unless we are ourselves very strict and emphasize the distribution of your books.

We think that any success attained here, despite the miserable and hard circumstances of this country, is because somehow or other we’ve received your mercy and blessings. Now we have the big responsibility of not only maintaining your project but also making it grow. Please keep accepting us as your humble servants and never deprive us of the shelter of your lotus feet.

Your servants in Lima, Peru,  
(written by Dāru Kṛṣṇa dasa)

Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Dāru-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Dharmānanda dāsa / Vāsudeva-parāyaṇa dāsa

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust at your lotus feet.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for …  
Giving us transcendental sound vibrations  
Caring to save the fallen nations,  
Describing the Lord’s holy name and form,  
Shining your light in this Kali-yuga storm,  
Showing us the perfect way to live,  
Giving us all that you had to give,  
Telling us that Kṛṣṇa wears a yellow dhotī,  
Being the example of a perfect devotee,  
Tolerating many difficult times in New York,  
Holding our hands as we stumble and learn to walk,  
Being the personification of all good qualities and actions,  
Showing us perfection in your dealings and business transactions,  
Bringing about the revolution predicted in the Bhāgavatam,  
Revealing the words kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam,  
The secret weapons of prasādam and books,  
Personally showing us how to cook,  
Bringing Lord Caitanya’s saṅkīrtana movement to this world,  
Your ISKCON emblem—the lotus flower with petals unfurled.

Your servants in Liverpool, England,  
(written by Cakranādī-devī dāsī)

## Ljubljana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We are very happy to have the opportunity to write you an offering for your Vyāsa-pūjā day. Our Ljubljana center is in Slovenia, one of the parts of Yugoslavia. This is one of the remote parts of the world that got the mercy of the Lord only due to your causeless mercy and superhuman endeavors. We heard one lecture wherein you said that even in such a place as Yugoslavia people are very gladly taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness and chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. This refers to the fact that Yugoslavia was a Communist country and people here had very little opportunity to practice spiritual life. But now the impossible has happened—the Communist regime is finished, although due to the influence of Kali-yuga the opposite was to be expected: that Communists would prevail over the capitalists, or, in other words, that *śūdras* would prevail in the world and destroy everything human.

We understand that the fall of the Communist regimes throughout Eastern Europe is due only to your transcendental Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, which is pouring down the rain of mercy on the poor conditioned souls of this iron age. You have taken us out of thick illusion and given us the light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Who can understand your mercy? You have given us the most wonderful books on Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and these are our life and soul. By reading these books, we feel that you are personally present. These books give us spiritual strength to go on in the execution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. When we read them, we also develop the desire to distribute them. And people are taking these books. Although they have less and less money, since the Kali-yuga *rākṣaṣas* are plundering them, nevertheless our book sales are increasing more and more. We feel that you are satisfied, and in this way we are satisfied. These books are Kṛṣṇa. You gave us Kṛṣṇa. How can we ever repay you?

You also gave us a whole lifestyle of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that we do not need to speculate on how to live, how to eat, how to sleep, what to do.… You saved our lives. You saved us. Please let us always stay under the shelter of your divine lotus feet and be happy eternally. Don’t allow *māyā* to ever overcome us. And even if we decide to leave Kṛṣṇa consciousness out of foolishness and rascaldom, please kindly pull us back by the hair and punish us properly.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our spiritual master, our spiritual grandfather, and it is understood that the grandfather is generally more merciful to his grandchildren than their father. Therefore give us your special mercy to go on with your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness and to finally go home, back to Godhead, after finishing this lifetime.

Your neophyte disciples in Ljubljana, Yugoslavia,

Akṣaya dāsa / Avadhūta dāsa / Dhanañjaya dāsa / Vālī-priya dāsa / Bhakta Andrej / Bhakta Darko / Bhakta Mirko / Bhakta Miso / Vimalā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Irena / Bhaktin Lili / Bhaktin Natasa / Bhaktin Petra / Bhaktin Suzana

## London (south)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Another year has passed in separation from your physical presence, but you are still with us through your divine instructions, and by your unlimited mercy you have allowed us to continue serving you and your dearmost disciples. In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Antya-līlā* 2.13–14) it is stated,

*tā-sabā tārite prabhu sei saba deśe*  
*yogya-bhakta jīva-dehe karena ‘āveśe’*  
*sei jīve nija-bhakti karena prakāśe*  
*tāhāra darśane ‘vaiṣṇava’ haya sarva-deśe*

**“To deliver people in regions throughout the universe who could not meet Him, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally entered the bodies of pure devotees. Thus He empowered living beings \[His pure devotees\] by manifesting in them so much of His own devotion that people in all other countries became devotees by seeing them.”**

Hence we can understand, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are such a personality. We were all wandering hopelessly through millions of different bodies, searching in vain for some lasting pleasure, and becoming frustrated at every step. Seeing us in such a pitiful condition, Lord Caitanya sent us our only hope—you.

But to give us fallen souls the nectar of the holy name, you yourself had to go through so many difficulties. When you first started your fledging ISKCON in New York, you persevered through so much austerity, taking every situation to be Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. Your exemplary behavior was a lesson to us all, and we pray that we will be able to develop just an iota of your dedication to enable us to spread your glories all over South London.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally indebted to you, and the purpose of our existence is only to attempt to repay this debt. On this auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we can only fall at your lotus feet and beg forgiveness for our countless offenses. We humbly ask to be forever engaged in expanding this movement, birth after birth, and can only hope that our insignificant effort gives you some transcendental pleasure.

Your insignificant granddisciples in South London,  
(written by Rādhā-Dāmodara-devī dāsī)

## London (city)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

You are lord and master, savior of the universe. You are king of kings, lion among men, sage among the demigods. Who can fathom your inestimable glories? By your divine touch bell metal becomes gold, animals become human, hippies become happies, and punks become monks. In the story of Dhruva Mahārāja you explain how the boy became so powerful that simply by the pressure of his big toe the entire earth was moved. In a purport you state that this is the glory of the pure devotee. Wherever he goes, everyone and everything is spiritualized.

Of course, your greatest glory is that somehow we’re still engaged in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Despite our madness, you keep us connected to the Lord. What is that connection? Devotional service. In the *Prabhupāda Nectar* series we read how Śrutakīrti Prabhu once told you he was disturbed because he felt he had no love for you. He explained how dozens of devotees were chanting, dancing, and crying in ecstasy when you would arrive, but somehow he could not participate in such feelings. Later you asked him, “So, do you like serving me?”

“Oh, yes, Prabhupāda,” he replied, “I like serving you very much.”

“Then that is love,” you said. “Everyone can do so many things—singing, dancing, jumping up and down—but you are actually doing something. Isn’t this love?”

Brahmatīrtha Prabhu gives a nice account in *Prabhupāda Nectar* as well. He explained how in the beginning he was challenging, but in the end you made him surrender, not by coercion but by love. He was convinced that you related to him as he related to you. When he was proud, you ignored him. When he was humble, you gave him your loving glance. We greatly relish stories like these, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we live them daily. When we’re proud, you chastise us in just the appropriate way. When we’re humble, you reward us with knowledge and a taste for humility.

You taught us that Vaiṣṇnava humbleness is the primary characteristic of one who knows the truth. Arrogance is the emblem of a fool. As His Holiness Lokanāth Swami once pointed out to us in London, *vidyā* (knowledge) must be followed by *vinaya* (humility). If *vidyā* is not followed by *vinaya,* the result is *ahaṅkāra* (false ego). Then that same *vidyā* will act like *avidyā* (ignorance). That’s why you hold back with us sometimes, Śrīla Prabhupāda—because you know how greedy we are. A little success and we become like the mosquito on the lap of the king. You explain so nicely how the mosquito is thinking himself to be the object of worship although the subjects have come to see the king. But with one slap the mosquito is finished. This is your greatness, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You’re purifying each of us in a unique and individualized way. When we follow your instructions, we can feel your pleasure. When we deviate, we feel your pressure. But in all circumstances, whether soft as a rose or hard as a thunderbolt, you’re loving us in a way we can barely appreciate. Spiritually we are children, but you are the role model. By your grace we can mold ourselves in your mood. Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for we need you now more than ever.

Your servants in London (Soho Street),  
(written by Raghunātha Dāsa dāsa)

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the anniversary of your appearance day in this world.

Your appearance in this world means that persons like ourselves, who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in this age of quarrel and hypocrisy, can become enlightened by the brilliant knowledge of the Absolute Truth, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You are presenting this knowledge to us in disciplic succession from Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the incarnation of Lord Kṛṣṇa who originally put transcendental knowledge of the aBsolute Truth into written scripture, such as the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Because you submissively received and fully imbibed this knowledge from your eternal spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and are firmly fixed in the service of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in accordance with the desires of your spiritual master, you are a transparent via medium through whom one can behold the Absolute Truth. As such, you are a direct representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, worthy to be honored on the same level as the Godhead Himself.

Therefore, your instructions are completely nondifferent from the eternal religious principles set forth by Śrīla Vyāsadeva in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and are the means by which we blind conditioned souls, entrapped by the material energy and thus having completely lost sight of the ultimate goal of life, can become free from this network of illusion and return home to the eternal spiritual kingdom, where we will perpetually exist in full bliss and knowledge in association with Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. As such, your books, lectures, etc., are direct manifestations of the Absolute Truth. They are pure spiritual sound.

No wonder you are able to spread the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service, especially the chanting and hearing of His holy names—in other words, the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya—throughout this planet in this degraded Age of Kali. Only one who is empowered by Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa through complete surrender to the instructions of his spiritual master, the present via medium, the “current link” in the chain of disciplic succession, can do like this. This you have shown by your personal example and are still showing through those who surrender to your divine teachings in disciplic succession. And you will continue to show this to future generations of devotees in this Kali-yuga for the next ten thousand years.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our ever well-wisher, our eternal spiritual father. Please bless us that we may actually become your humble servants by wholeheartedly submitting ourselves to your divine instructions without reservation. In this way, year after year, as long as we have life, we may come together and try to appreciate you, our life blood, and try to spread about your wonderful, exemplary character and your glorious activities, renowned throughout the three worlds. Then one day, after having genuinely and sincerely served and pleased you in full Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we shall by your mercy return home to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s abode.

Your aspiring servants here at ISKCON Longdenville pray that this Vyāsa-pūjā offering is to your satisfaction.

Your servants at ISKCON Longdenville, Trinidad,  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-śaraṇa dāsa)

Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Kṛṣṇa-śaraṇa dāsa

## Madison Heights

Most dear Gurudeva, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Our Vyāsa-pūjā offering is an English translation of a Bengali poem given to you in 1971 by a famous Bengali poet named Shantasil Dāsa, from Panihati, West Bengal. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you said you liked this poem very much and wanted it printed in the Bengali BTG. So we hope this English translation is as pleasing to you:

You have given the supreme love of Godhead to the world, constantly preaching unconditionally the name of Kṛṣṇa without considering time, place, or person. Now the holy name is rising everywhere, country to country, in so many languages. With both your hands raised, you distributed the *mahā-mantra* throughout your whole life. All glories, all glories to the holy names of Śrī Kṛṣṇa given by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! These holy names are saving the suffering world from the threefold miseries. Let the whole world chant of the holy names by your mercy. Then the darkness will dissipate and the thirsty, suffering heart will be quenched by drinking the ever-refreshing nectar of this *mahā-mantra*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, again and again we offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Your servants in Madison Heights and Royal Oak, Michigan,

Prabhāsa dāsa / Viśālākṣī-devī dāsī / Indrabhartā dāsa / Vaikuṇṭha-sundarī-devī dāsī / Kiśorī-priyā-devī dāsī / Prema-bhakti dāsa / Rādhikā-devī dāsī / Dīnadayādri-devī dāsī / Narottama dāsa

## Manchester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine self and your divine mission, ISKCON.

After some years in spiritual life, it is all too easy to become complacent about our *sādhana*, about our service, about Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, and about our very great fortune in having been picked up from the material ocean of misery. Such complacence slips almost unheeded into our attitude, and we then become a nice target for Māyā, who never misses a trick. The big hand swings out and we are slapped down and floundering on the seat of our *dhotī* before we even know it.

However, such complacence vanishes when we meditate on what you went through to save us. Your preaching success is a tremendous and stunning achievement. Even now it is surely hardly appreciated among the masses of people, yet for us the wonderment just goes on increasing. For so many reasons, your endeavor was truly magnificent. Recently we spoke to a life member who is seventy years old. When he thinks how, at that same age, you started out from India to begin this movement, he simply gapes in pure amazement. And we ourselves, as we struggle to achieve what sometimes seems to be the most impossible task—establishing just one small temple—can more and more realize the difficulties you had to overcome. But even our limited insight leaves us awestruck.

When you landed in America, what a sight greeted you there! Of course, you had heard stories about the degradation in the West, but could you have imagined the unmentionable depths of ignorance that prevailed in that unfortunate land? What we were! And what you are! The designations used to describe a person’s worldly status no longer have any relevance in your case. Beyond the topmost Āryan, you can only be known as a *paramahaṁsa*—one of those swanlike transcendentalists not of this world, and who this world hardly ever sees. But you looked upon us—the *antyajas,* the *mlecchas,* the *caṇḍālas*—without any envy but with pure compassion, and you gave yourself completely to the thankless task of saving us.

And indeed you were successful: we were saved. And even more amazingly, because of you we are now saving others. But we are lazy and forgetful; we need your constant upbraidment. We are so prone to complacence, but still you somehow always bring us back. You have saved us, are saving us now, and will go on saving us. All this has been achieved through ISKCON, the great manifestation of Kṛṣṇa’s causeless mercy upon us and the glorification of you, His pure devotee.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow us to remain a part of ISKCON. Perhaps we do not yet understand what ISKCON is, perhaps the whole world is already a part of this divine limb of Lord Caitanya. But we fear losing the association of the devotees, those who are firmly fixed at your lotus feet. Just a tiny amount of advancement is enough to make us so proud and complacent. Deluded by misconceptions of our own spiritual standing, we neglect your order and the order of your servants. That is the danger: pride stalks us like a fearsome predator, waiting to tear apart our puny devotion at any moment. Please protect us from that, O lord; you are our only hope. Fix us in the service of your servants and followers. Dear lord, as the king himself comes out to kill the lion, please kill off all vestiges of our foolish, unwarranted pride.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are proud, but you know the truth. Simply by the moonlike rays shining from your heart, our passionate, lusty desires have been cooled, and we have felt the beginning of an attachment for Kṛṣṇa. We have joined the *saṅkīrtana* movement and are feeling all our material pangs diminishing. We thus pray that by your continued association we may reach the perfectional stage. Then our real, eternal service to Kṛṣṇa can resume, by your grace.

Thus upon this glorious day we must fall prostrate and offer our deepest thanks and obeisances. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants in Manchester, England,

Kṛṣṇa-dharma dāsa / Abhay dāsa / Cintāmaṇi-dhāma-devī dāsī / Bhakta Premji / Bhakta Mike / Bhata Berni / Bhakta Graham / Bhakta Chris / Bhaktin Irene / Bhaktin Marie

## Manipur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost eternal father, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your divine lotus feet. On this day of your appearance anniversary, we would like to recall your important instructions. Your lotus feet are our only shelter within the three worlds, for only they can save us from the clutches of birth, death, old age, and diseases. You are the representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as a *śaktyāveśa avatāra—*one who descends to this earth to fulfill the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. As said in *Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad* (6.23),

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**“Only unto those great souls who simultaneously have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of the Vedic knowledge automatically revealed.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our senses are so imperfect and our sources are so limited that it is not possible for us to have even the slightest knowledge of the absolute region without surrendering ourselves at your divine lotus feet.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, how shall we, the fallen devotees of ISKCON Manipur, repay the debt we owe you for the *kalpa-vṛkṣa* of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement you planted twenty-five years ago, giving the nectarean fruit of the holy name to those who take shelter under the shade of that desire tree, which stretches its branches to all parts of the world.

We are the most fallen of the fallen, having no intelligence, wealth, or strength worth the name to offer you. Therefore all we can do is seek shelter under your lotus feet. We have started the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandra Maṇi-mandira project, which will cost more than fifty million rupees. His Holiness Bhakti Svarūpa Dāmodara Swami laid the foundation stone of the Maṇi-Mandira on Friday, March 22, 1991 (the 7th day of the month of Caitra) at 10:30 a.m., the most auspicious moment of the day. The project has been dedicated to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa for your divine satisfaction. We sincerely pray that you bless us and give us enough strength to complete it by 1996, when we will offer it to your lotus feet as a part of the centennial celebration of your divine appearance being observed all over the world.

Ever dependent on your mercy, we beg to remain,

Your worthless servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandra Maṇi-Mandira, Manipur, India,

## Mauritius

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your feet. All glories to your holy appearance day.

As the years go by, our remembrance of you is increasing. We now have all the books you wrote, we have your *Līlāmṛta* and your *Nectar* books, we have your recorded lectures and your transcendental songs, we have your letters and your *Festival* book, and we also have your conversations, which are like the icing on the cake. We are fortunate to be able to delve into that ocean of literature and remain enlivened and spiritually inspired by this. Then, as you said, “Little thing: follow strictly—where is the difficulty?”

The difficulty is that we are not serious enough to actually do as you said. There is always some kind of deviation, some kind of spirit of sense gratification, either gross or subtle, waiting to pull us away from your remembrance. And according to how much we forget you, to that degree we are impure, and this is where we get stuck in our advancement.

It is the duty of the disciple to appeal to the spiritual master to save him. The spiritual, being *karuṇā-sindhu adhama janāra bandhu*—the ocean of mercy and the friend of the poor (“poor” in this sense means the spiritually weak and bankrupt)—will help his sincere disciple overcome the difficulties and weaknesses that stop him from advancing in spiritual life. But the appeal has to be sincere, just like the helpless crying of a child for its mother.

Of course, there is no specific time this has to be done; rather, the disciple always feels himself helpless and in need of Śrī Guru’s mercy.

But definitely on the occasion of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā we feel how we are like insignificant glowworms in front of you. We therefore appeal for your merciful glance upon us on this auspicious day and beg for your kindness to keep us engaged in your service. We shall do our best to follow your instructions strictly, to cooperate as you desired, and to come to a higher level of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We hope in this way we can please you, and once you are pleased Kṛṣṇa will also be pleased and let us approach more and more. But without your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we’ll become mice again.

Your insignificant servants on the ISKCON Vedic Farm in Vṛndāvana, Mauritius,

Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Śrīniketana dāsa / Arjuna dāsa
