## Nova Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the year 1965, when you arrived in the United States, you wrote a poem. One stanza in this poem is as follows:

*rajas tamo guṇe erā saboi ācchanna*  
*vāsudeb-kathā ruci nāhi se prasanna*

Here you express your doubt that the people of the West would listen to Vāsudeva’s messages, absorbed as they are in the modes of passion and ignorance. These were the messages you were bringing forth in your Bhaktivedanta purports. You were fulfilling the desire of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, by writing books and converting Western people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Although you were worried that people would have difficulty understanding your profound teachings due to their absorption in the mode of passion, you were completely dependent on Kṛṣṇa and forged ahead fearlessly.

Now, twenty five years have gone by. You have seen a great family of devotees grow, all of them endowed with an incomparable love for you. A little before going back to Kṛṣṇa, you revealed to us: “All I had to tell you is in my books. I will always live in my books.” You also asked us to live together, without fighting, working with love and confidence. This is also related to the practice of hearing your purports. It is related because in the Seventh Chapter of the *Kṛṣṇa* book you say that as a result of hearing about Kṛṣṇa one gains the right of associating with devotees; and as a result of associating with devotees one is able to control the mind. You comment about this when explaining the meaning of the song beginning *bhaja huṅre mana*. And in the sixth chapter of the *Bhagavad-gītā* you describe the result of controlling the mind as follows: “The constitutional position of the living entity is to carry out the superior’s order.… Mind has to admit a superior order and follow it.…”

In conclusion, by hearing you describe and glorify the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, in your Bhaktivedanta purports, we became worthy of associating with devotees. As a result of this we can control the mind and, consequently, accept a superior order and follow it. And all devotees know this order: “If you have love for me, then show me this by being able to work together.” Unfortunately, we can perceive the fate of those who cannot control the mind and who live a tremendous hallucination. “As long as one’s mind remains an unconquered enemy, one has to serve the dictations of lust, anger, avarice, illusion, etc.”(*Bhagavad-gītā* 6.6, purport).

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as long as a devotee remains a victim of anger, sex desire, and illusion, how can he work together with other devotees and show genuine love for you?

On this auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we, the inhabitants of Nova Gokula, beg from you a drop of your strength and determination so that we can absorb our minds in your Bhaktivedanta purports. After all, isn’t that what we ask every day when we sing for you *guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya, āra nā koriho mane āśā*? *Guru-mukha-padma-vākya* refer to the words that emanate from your lotus mouth. And in this song we’re asking that we may understand and become absorbed in these words of yours and not desire to hear any others. But your words are overflowing from hundreds and thousands of Bhaktivedanta purports. Obviously, one who compares the effort of studying your purports to the process of *jñāna-yoga* or acquiring academic knowledge is in the same category as one who compares the distribution of your books, *saṅkīrtana,* with selling ordinary, mundane books. Your purports are the words that emanate from your lotus mouth, and you have sacrificed your sleep to dictate these purports.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for extinguishing the fire caused by our mental concoctions and our subjective hallucinations. Thank you for convincing us, gradually, that we are the servants of the servant of Kṛṣṇa and that our only duty is satisfying you by satisfying Lord Kṛṣṇa’s senses.

Today we beg from you the strength to distribute this knowledge to the people whom, in one way or another, we come in touch with.

The eternal servants of your servants in the Nova Gokula farm community, Pindamanhangaba, Brazil.

## Oslo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

When you first entered the Western world with your suitcases of books in 1965, our entire civilization immediately started to shake in its boots, which until then had been steadily and swiftly walking toward hell. Suddenly there was a rapidly increasing attraction for subjects, ideals, and values that were not very much in the line of the program of the demons. Even up here in the north we could sense the repercussions of your entrance, although at that time we didn’t understand the significance of what was really going on, and even though we didn’t get to know about you until many years later. Materialistic poets were announcing a change of time, a new age, sensing the change in the course of history you were about to inaugurate. Practically nobody understood what was going on, why it was happening, or whence came the force for change.

However, now we know it was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who had come with your books to deliver the merciful rays of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi’s *saṅkīrtana* movement and bring about a revolution in the hearts of us impious souls residing in the lands of voidism and impersonalism. You came and dug a channel so that the water from the ocean of devotion could enter and make desertlike hearts into fertile ground for the plant of devotion to take dust from your lotus feet.

It is now almost fourteen years since you left this planet, and one may ask, Where is your mercy now? Where is the dust of your lotus feet? A similar question was asked by the sages of Naimiṣāraṇya after Kṛṣṇa had left the planet. The answer is also similar: Just as Sūta Gosvāmī explained that Kṛṣṇa had remained in the form of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* so you proclaimed that you would never die but remain living in the purports of your books. You were so concerned that after your departure people could continue to get your mercy. Therefore you created ISKCON and the BBT and emphasized again and again the production and distribution of your books in all the languages of the world. You could not tolerate seeing any living entity suffer because of forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa.

When I read your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* for the first time (even before it dawned on me that the Supreme Lord Himself was speaking) I was struck by the fact that this book was written and spoken by personalities who cared about me, the reader. And that compassion was even more attractive than the depth of the philosophy. This compassionate and caring mood is not only manifested in your books, but also through your disciples, who represent various opulences of your personality.

Besides arranging carefully that your mercy remain available, you also gave clear instructions on how to take it and keep it. You asked us to give it to others by spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement, especially by distributing your books. Although we who are your granddisciples never had the privilege of arranging for your preaching, because of your books we can carry you out to the people every day so that you can preach in every town and village all over the world. In this way our preaching can have great potency, although we are so unqualified and impure. Even in this tiny, frozen Christian country the percentage of people who believe in the transmigration of the soul has increased from twelve percent to thirty-three percent in a few years. We know why.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we consider it the perfection of our lives to assist our spiritual master in arranging for your preaching through your books and lectures. Sometimes somebody may appreciate what we are speaking, and that may be inspiring, but if they take one of your books, then it is perfection. Then we know they are saved.

So on this most auspicious day of your appearance we beg for your blessings to continue serving Kṛṣṇa within your ISKCON and to center our lives on your books: reading them, living by them, and distributing them. Then we know you will be present with us.

All glories to you, the most powerful distributor of Kṛṣṇa consciousness!

The servants of your servants in Oslo, Norway,

Tapas dāsa / Tilakanātha dāsa / Praṇava dāsa / Rukmiṇī-ramaṇa dāsa / Acintya-māyā dāsa / Haṁsa-vapur dāsa / Bhakta Sveinung / Bhakta William / Bhakta Mark / Bhakta Paal / Vaiṣṇavī-devī dāsī / Kaṁsārī-devī dāsī / Kālindī-devī dāsī

## Ottawa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**“Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Most merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances, and thank you for giving us shelter at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us, and indeed all mankind, the most precious gifts. You have given us the knowledge of our true identity as eternal spirit souls, parts and parcels of Kṛṣṇa, our supreme father, master and well-wishing friend. You have given us the method by which we can re-establish our relationship with Lord Kṛṣṇa*—śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana*. You have given us the opportunity to associate directly with Lord Kṛṣṇa by means of His holy name and by worshiping His transcendental form, especially here in Ottawa, where we have the most beautiful and merciful forms of their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. You have increased your mercy by giving us the association of your dearmost devotees. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything in your ISKCON to make our lives perfect and to help others make their lives perfect.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā we pray that we remain under the shelter of your lotus feet and become worthy of your mercy, and that we may also extend your mercy to others. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble servants in Ottawa, Canada,

Śaṅkara dāsa / Nārada dāsa / Śānti-devī dāsī / Sītā-devī dāsī

Congregation

Jayendra Doshi and family / Vishnu Kapur and family / Soom Pandith and family / Kanshi Nath Puri and family / Maheswar Sahoo and family / Jugal Sharma and family / Nityanand Varma and family / Prem Varma and family / Peter Waclawaski and family / Leisa

## Padua

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the divine dust of your holy lotus feet.

By the mercy of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa you appeared in this material world to create an army of soldier-devotees entrusted with distributing the message of the sacred scriptures. Therefore you are the most dear devotee of the Lord, as confirmed in *Bhagavad-gītā.*

By following your instructions, the entire material world can be transformed into the spiritual world—such is the potency of your words. They are the active principle of spiritual life, as Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja writes in a verse of *Caitanya-caritāmṛta.*

During your presence you taught us in so many ways—in a very personal way—how to please the Supreme Person, how to serve the Lord, how to preach His glories. Since you were in direct contact with the spiritual energy and directly in touch with the Lord within your pure heart, you could instruct us all so perfectly that we became completely convinced of the presence of the Lord everywhere. You taught us spiritual science, and you taught us how to become fearless, humble, determined, and expert and to cultivate many other wonderful qualities—qualities you could infuse in us because they are fully within you.

As I read the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and discovered more and more the depth of your purports, I came to understand that as Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s pastimes are inconceivable and extremely profound, so the multifarious pastimes you performed to preach and instruct humanity about *bhakti-yoga* are also inconceivable and extremely profound.

Even though you are a great *mahā-bhāgavata,* you came among the most fallen and showed us the most simple and sublime method of self-realization—the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

Therefore you are celebrated as Mahā-patita-pāvana, the most compassionate and merciful savior of the fallen. And therefore we take great pleasure in writing this offering glorifying your holy personality.

There are many Godbrothers more qualified, more responsible, and more advanced than I am due to their having accepted the responsibility of following exactly your instructions and taking care that others also follow them. For many years they have carried on with this valuable service to your lotus feet, experiencing newer and newer realizations.

Still, although I know myself to be unqualified and materialistic, I pray to remain your humble servant and learn the art of associating with your pure disciples, assisting them, serving them, and appreciating with an adult understanding how they are sacrificing everything for ISKCON, not caring for personal comfort but always fixing their minds on the mission of their eternal spiritual master, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

I wish to learn in a more personal way how to glorify and remember your unlimited greatness. By this effort alone I can already feel your presence closer and closer.

As you said, “If you love me, I love you.” In this way I can only hope to increase my little love for you, and by your kind mercy and compassion, if you allow me, I may expand that love and advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness by distributing your books. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants in Padua, Italy.  
(written by Durgama dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine self.

When Prahlāda Mahārāja was offering his prayers to Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva, he described the nature and mood of a highly enlightened saintly person. It is a quite common practice for a person interested in spiritual advancement to go to the forest for his own liberation. Prahlāda Mahārāja says about himself, however, that he was not interested in being liberated alone and leaving other people in the midst of distress in the material world. According to Prahlāda Mahārāja, helping people in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the best method to please the Lord. A real saintly person feels compassion for others and wants to help them, and therefore he preaches the glories of the Lord for their sake. Thus Prahlāda Mahārāja remained in society simply to enlighten the people about devotional service to the Lord.

Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you preached the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness even at the risk of your spiritual peace and other comforts as well. It is due to your endeavor that today the world is witnessing pure devotees of Lord Caitanya distributing love of Godhead all over the surface of the earth.

Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have neither any hope nor any direction. Please bless us so that we may be of some use to you.

Your fallen servants in Pandharpur, India,

Caitanyacandra dāsa / Sītārāṇī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Deshmuk Kishore / Bhakta Avinas

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, my eternal master,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your louts feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to your ISKCON society!

Yet another year has passed, and I feel as if I have achieved so little in my attempt to reach the tip of your lotus feet. Over this last year this world has gone completely mad, and more mad it will continue to go. By the end of this century we expect the full manifestation of the symptoms of Kali-yuga, as described in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* by Śrīla Śukadeva Gosvāmī.

Sometimes it seems hopeless to try to make these people aware of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Māyā has very expertly spread her veil of illusion and ignorance over the whole planet. Sometimes it seems that she is forcibly preventing people from making spiritual advancement. Just as at night a ghost takes full possession of the mind and body of a person and creates havoc, so the modes of material nature are keeping everyone on a leash, commanding complete obedience.

To be born in this age is both a blessing and a curse. So many people are born in this age due to praying constantly to be able to participate in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya, and many people have simply fallen into this dark age due to their failure to make spiritual advancement in previous ages. So the choice is ours: to take advantage of the blessing and ignore the curse.

*kaler doṣa-nidhe rājan asti hy eko mahān guṇaḥ*  
*kīrtanād eva kṛṣṇasya mukta-saṅgaḥ param vrajet*

**“My dear King, although Kali-yuga is full of faults, there is still one good quality about this age: Simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa** ***mahā-mantra,*** **one can become free from material bondage and be promoted to the transcendental kingdom” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **12.3.51).**

Because *māyā* seems so real, strong, and overwhelming, we always think that everything is O.K. and that it will all be rectified in due course of time. Now they are talking about “a new world order,” which will allow all humans to engage happily in unrestricted sense gratification by keeping everyone’s territory under full supervision, just to make sure that no one will interfere with society’s collective suicide. How wonderful … They may have their plans, and we have our plans, but Kṛṣṇa has His plans.

There is no doubt now that the battle with Māyā is full on. Already many soldiers, some of our best fighters, *mahārathīs,* even generals, have fallen head first into the grip of that witch. She is present everywhere, as much as air and ether are filling up the space between planets. She’s even within our minds, dancing and playing in our intelligence.

When a devotee fights against her every day of his life and kicks her from all sides with *harināma, prasādam, sādhu,* and *śāstra,* he wonders, “Will Māyā ever go away for good?” Actually, since we are in Māyā’s kingdom, there is no hope of her going. Rather, it is we who should make plans to leave her kingdom and never return.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, answering the prayers of the *ācāryas* and your Guru Mahārāja, you came to the West in 1965 and announced to the whole world at large that a boat is leaving Kali-yuga for the supreme abode, Goloka Vṛndāvana. And since then you have continued to announce it—in every city, village, and home and on every street corner. You have announced it in every language and arranged for tickets to be sold.

*Bṛhat mṛdaṅga,* your best travel agent.

Every book you translated provides all color pictures of the destination to reach.

*Bṛhat mṛdaṅga,* your best advertisement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the best of all human beings that ever took birth in this material world.

We are so small and insignificant, and you are so great and powerful. How can we ever measure up to your stature? Still, you are so compassionate. So many mistakes we make, so many times we fail in our duty to serve you. So often we neglect your instructions. It’s like we are not really convinced about getting on the boat and sailing away across this dark ocean. And if we decide to jump on the boat, after a while, because the waves are tossing a little too hard, we want to jump off and swim our way back to the shore, back to zero.

So you remain compassionate with your children who are learning how to walk. You bear the same fruits that Lord Nityānanda bestowed upon the most fallen, and thus your compassion and tolerance know no bounds.

This ISKCON movement is like sugar candy. From any side you taste it, it tastes sweet, even to those with the worst cases of jaundice. That’s because you always keep your eyes on us. You are our ever well-wisher.

Māyā is undoubtedly very strong. Otherwise why is it so hard to attend *maṅgala ārati* every day? So hard to stay awake, to hear, to chant, to serve, to be humble, and, most difficult, to remember Kṛṣṇa? Nonetheless, the life of a devotee is so easy and sublime, so sweet and ever fresh. It always moistens the heart, as the milk of the Surabhi cows always keeps the pasturing grounds wet and muddy.

“Behave or don’t preach,” you said in your class in Vṛndāvana. How can we expect the people to follow the principles if we don’t follow them? How can we expect them to have tears in their eyes at the sound of holy name when we have such a hard time finishing our rounds? Can we still make plans to take over the world? You wanted ideal Vaiṣṇavas. An ideal Vaiṣṇava is one who never gives up under any circumstances.

So how can we do that? I can only see one solution: To never lose sight of your instructions. To never stop studying your books. To never stop chanting and dancing. To never stop humbling ourselves by constantly paying our respects to everyone. To be ready to accept any menial service, even when situated at the top rank. To never stop preaching and distributing your books.

As long as we never stop, Māyā will not catch us. As long as we keep on going in the right direction, we will be able to close our ears to the nagging of Māyā. As long as we exclusively choose just you and Kṛṣṇa, one day we will reach the shelter of your lotus feet.

**A Few Words in Praise of His Divine Grace**

Seated on a large lotus flower,  
His swanlike head held erect,  
His tilaka perfectly drawn on his forehead,  
A yellow rose garland around his neck,  
His soft pink lotus feet resting  
On a cloud of Kadamba flower petals,  
His eyes full of tears and joy looking at Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa,  
His oceanic smile that could contain the whole universe,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda is waiting for us all.  
Far above the darkness of this world,  
He has made his eternal residence at the feet of the Lord.  
Let our mind, our body, our words be with him constantly,  
That at the time of departure, when this carcass is worth nothing,  
We can look up and lift our arms to him,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord and master.

The servants of your servants in Perth, Australia.  
(written by Yadunātha dāsa)

## Philadelphia

Dear Srila Prabhupada,Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this day of your Vyāsa-pūjā we want to glorify you, our spiritual savior, and beg your forgiveness for our innumerable offenses throughout this past year.

As a pure and gentle devotee of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, you have superexcellently exhibited the devotional principle of *guru-sevā* by fulfilling Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī’s instructions to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western countries. As Lord Kṛṣṇa tells Arjuna in the Ādi Purāṇa, “One who says he is My devotee is not My devotee, but one who says he is the devotee of My devotee—he is My devotee.” Thus we who are groping for light in the darkness of our cavernous material desires can perceive through *śāstra-cakṣus* what a pristinely pure and sparkling devotee you are.

Although you have made the essence of truth and religion easily understandable and attainable through the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* I think we must be very offensive to not appreciate more fully the shelter you are offering, considering how truly perilous and flimsy our situation is. Please forgive us and continue to look favorably upon us so that we may ever-increasingly realize the absolute necessity of grabbing hold of your lotus feet as we toss about day after day, year after year, life after miserable life in this ocean of *māyā*. Kindly bless us so that we may appreciate more and more our sublime good fortune, and the cause of that good fortune, Your Divine Grace, our eternal well-wisher and spiritual father.

We offer our prostrated obeisances hundreds of thousands of times to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in gratefulness for your giving us so much facility to help us revive our love of God—facility in the shape of your books, which illumine our hearts, and *prasādam,* which engages our tongues and saves us from the hellish sins of meat-eating. Thank you for the Deity worship, which soothes our minds and dispels the ill winds of impersonalism. Thank you for the opportunity to engage all our senses in devotional service by assisting you in spreading Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, which is like a multifaceted spiritual gem shining rays of divine hope for all the conditioned souls to bathe in and become refreshed.

We can never claim to appreciate enough what you have given us, nor can we ever repay you. You have told us that only a fool would ever think he has done enough for his spiritual master. Your eternal instruction to us as your initiated disciples and granddisciples, and to all those who seek your shelter for the next ten thousand years, is that the way to reciprocate with you for all the knowledge and spiritual gifts you have given is to focus our energies in working cooperatively to assist you in spreading Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, while simultaneously following all the regulative principles and devotional practices you have prescribed.

This process of working together within ISKCON is also the way of developing a taste for the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. As stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.2.16), “By serving the pure devotees great service is done, and one gains a taste for the transcendental message of Godhead.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, not only have you exhaustively explained for us the goal of life and the process of achieving it, but you have given us the one and only process that will enable living entities completely averse to the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to regain their lost taste for Kṛṣṇa consciousness and thus ultimately be freed from the cycle of *saṁsāra*.

What a horrible condition of life, to be averse to the most valuable and potent medicine! How terrible to not even know that one is diseased with the cancer of spiritual ignorance! How many countless living entities in the human form are asleep to their spiritual identities? How much are those souls suffering because of this ignorance? Therefore you told us out of your pure devotion and deep spiritual compassion that we must preach, preach, preach this nonsectarian science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to our fallen brothers and sisters, for that will do good both for them and for us.

On this day of your most blessed Vyāsa-pūjā day we pray to you that we may always be cooperatively and enthusiastically engaged as your responsible spiritual children in our family business, in your devotional business of saving both ourselves and as many of our fallen family members as we possibly can through the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you have given us.

*harer nāma harer nāma harer namaiva kevalam*  
*kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā*

**“In the Age of Kali there is no other way, no other way, no other way of spiritual deliverance except for the chanting of the holy names, the chanting of the holy names, the chanting of the holy names of Lord Hari.”**

Your servants in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania,  
(written by Dīna-śaraṇa dāsa)

## Porto Alegre

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You have given us the chance to develop our love for Kṛṣṇa, and also the opportunity to join Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* party, to chant and dance and take *prasādam*.

Our spiritual lives have been formed by your mercy and careful guidance.

Please continue to sustain our Kṛṣṇa conscious lives. It is true that simply if we follow your instructions, everything is accomplished. The living entities are wandering in this material world through different species of life from time immemorial, but only due to the mercy of Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master does the spirit soul get the seed of devotional service.

We are so much indebted to you. We can’t glorify you enough, for your greatness is unlimited. Out of compassion and mercy you came to plant the seed of devotional service. The only way we can ever begin to repay you is to beg to always remain humble servants of your lotus feet and to continue to carry out your instructions.

We see that through your constant preaching efforts people all over the world are associating with the devotees and with Your Divine Grace through your books, your transcendental gifts to the world.

So today we pray to you: Please enable us to be fully engaged in your service, and please bestow upon us the ability to increase our preaching spirit for book distribution and *harināma-saṅkīrtana*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we take shelter of your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

Your servants in Porto Alegre, Brazil,

## Portugal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories unto Your Divine Grace.

Please kindly accept this insignificant attempt to try to glorify your unlimited mercy upon us.

**Prabhupāda-kṛpa**

**Part I**

Just as mother Ganges is flowing from the spiritual world through the hole in the universal shell made by the lotus toe of Lord Viṣṇu to save the fallen souls in the material world, so the mercy of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is flowing from the spiritual world through Your Divine Grace to save the fallen souls of the Kali-yuga. Just as the Ganges first touches the head of Lord Śiva, the greatest of all Vaiṣṇavas, before touching the earth, so Lord Caitanya’s mercy, coming through you, first comes to your faithful disciples, our spiritual masters, before it spreads to the ordinary devotees and general mass of people.

And just as the merciful Ganges never stops flowing and is always sweeter and sweeter, so your kind mercy is also unlimited and tastes always more and more nectarean to all.

But there are also some differences:

Unlike the Ganges, your mercy is not confined to a certain tract of land and to the people that go after it. You are kindly distributing it widely, all over the world, and it goes to where the people are, even if in the beginning they’re reluctant to take it.

Also, your mercy doesn’t have only periodic overflows that inundate the regions around it; rather, it is always overflowing and causing a general flood all over the world that is going to last for at least ten thousand years.

All glories to your kind mercy, which is taking us back to the spiritual world in a flood of love of Godhead!

**Part II**

When Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī met Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in Prayag, he addressed Him as the most munificent of all incarnations—the *mahā-vadanya avatāra*. If Rūpa Gosvāmī would have met you in New York or London or Moscow or Mexico or. . . how would he have addressed you? What is the expression for “even more magnanimous and more merciful than Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself”?

**Part III**

Lord Caitanya’s tree of mercy has many branches and sub-branches, as described by Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī. But the main and biggest branch, growing directly from the main trunk and from Lord Nityānanda, the branch that is becoming more and more prominent day after day and whence the main stream of Lord Caitanya’s mercy is flowing—that is your ISKCON, Śrīla Prabhupāda. This most merciful branch of Lord Caitanya’s tree is taking over the whole world and growing in all directions. It’s giving life to the rest of the tree, making it glow and blossom, and the tree’s tasty sweet fruits are becoming even more nectarean than ever before.

All glories to your merciful creation, your masterpiece, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, which is saving the whole world!

**Part IV**

The six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana are celebrated for having established eternal religious principles for the benefit of all human beings through their writings and studies. Thus they are honored all over the three worlds.

We’re bereft of words to describe your most merciful writings and your Bhaktivedanta purports. We and the rest of humanity are not only being saved from hell but are going back to our eternal home, back to Godhead, thanks to your books. On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, we bow down at your lotus feet millions and millions of times in an act of gratitude for your kind mercy.

Your insignificant servants in Portugal,

Rādhā-Govinda dāsa / Śacī-suta dāsa / Kāraṇārṇava dāsa / Bhakta Mario / Bhakta Luis / Bhakta Artur / Bhakta Eddie / Bhakta Carios / Akleśa-devī dāsī / Gopāla-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Fati / Bhaktin Leonor

Children

Rafael / Hari / Jair / Vraja-rāja

## Puerto Rico

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this, the most auspicious day of your appearance, we come before your lotus feet praying for your mercy, praying that you forgive our offenses and kindly engage us in your service. The material energy is very powerful. Its influence forces the living entities into forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa, life after life.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prāsade pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

The conditioned living entities are suffering, but if by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy a fortunate living entity meets a pure devotee and renders some service to him, then the seed of *bhakti,* by the mercy of the pure devotee, is implanted in his heart.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, under the instruction of your spiritual master you came to the Western part of the world and distributed Kṛṣṇa’s mercy to everyone who came. Then you went back to Kṛṣṇa’s abode. The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* says that the saintly devotees who cross the ocean of material existence on the boat of Kṛṣṇa consciousness leave that boat on this side of the ocean so that others can also cross it. Śrīla Prabhupāda, the boat in which you crossed is still there—ISKCON. Anyone who wants to go back to Godhead can just get on board; then the vast ocean of material existence will be reduced to the insignificant amount of water contained in the footprint of a calf:

*samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ*  
*mahat-padaṁ puṇya-yaśo murāreḥ*  
*bhavāmbudhir vatsa-padaṁ paraṁ padaṁ*  
*padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the experienced captain of this boat. Please take us back home.

Your servants in Puerto Rico,  
(written by Viprahita dāsa)

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

How it would be nice  
if we all could realize  
your greatness and your purity  
by living a truly simple life.

On this auspicious day  
there is no better way  
to show you our sincerity  
than to take a pledge and say:

“I want to chant my rounds (16)  
(and) four regulative principles follow,  
Bhāgavatam and Gītā daily hear,  
(and) service to guru and Gaurāṅga render.”

These are your gifts to us,  
priceless and full of love.  
With them we shall decorate  
our almost useless lives.

And if someone has not yet found happiness,  
then try to go out with trust and kindness,  
meeting people in the streets and homes,  
and everyone’s troubles surely will be gone.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you again and again.

Your servants in Rome, Italy,  
(written by Harideva dāsa)

## Saltillo

*Rājovāca*  
*samīcīnaṁ vaco brahman*  
*sarva-jñasya tavānagha*  
*tamo viśīryate mahyaṁ*  
*hareḥ kathayataḥ kathām*

**“Mahārāja Parīkṣit said: O learned** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **you know everything because you are without material contamination. Therefore whatever you have spoken to me appears right. Your speeches are gradually destroying the darkness of my ignorance, for you are narrating the topics of the Lord” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **2.4.5).**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances. O divine master! Only by your causeless mercy can we become fully dedicated to Lord Caitanya’s mission. What is this mission? In a letter written in San Francisco in 1968 you stated, “We want to open hundreds of centers so that people may take up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is our mission.” And in your sacred purports to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (7.14.27–33) you write,

There are many atheists who oppose the worship of the Deity of the Supreme Personality of Godhead in the temple. In this verse, however, it is authoritatively stated that any place where the Deity is worshiped is transcendental; it does not belong to the material world.… Therefore, in the temple there must be the Deity of the Lord, and the Lord should be worshiped by the devotees. This combination of the devotees and Deities creates a first-class transcendental place. The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement invites everyone from all over the world, without discrimination as to caste or creed, to come to its centers and cultivate spiritual life perfectly.  
\[Here\] stress is given to one point: *harer arcāśritāś ca ye* or *harer arcā.* In other words, any place where the Deity of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is worshiped by devotees is most significant. The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is giving the population of the entire world a chance to take advantage of Kṛṣṇa consciousness through the ISKCON centers, where one may perform Deity worship and chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and in this way obtain results with effectiveness increased a thousand times. This constitutes the best welfare activity for human society. This was Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission as it was predicted by Him in the *Caitanya-bhāgavata* (*Antya* 4.126):

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra hoibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu wanted the Hare Krishna movement, with installed Deities, to spread to every village and town in the world, so that everyone in the world might take advantage of this movement and become all-auspicious in spiritual life.

Today, O topmost *ācārya,* this prediction is a fact. By your mercy we are constructing the new Rādhā-Govinda temple. But you cautioned us, “Constructing a big building requires great energy, and a devotee does not take to such business if it does not benefit him by advancing his devotional service. He may construct a temple for the Lord, and for that he may take all kinds of anxiety, but he does not construct a big house for his personal relations” (*Bhagavad-gītā* 12.16, purport).

So this is the reality. Māyā is giving us so many anxieties and tests through her agents, even in the form of so-called swamis and holy men (as you state in your books), but if you bestow your unlimited mercy on us, your most insignificant children, surely all these impediments will go away and we can become instruments in Lord Caitanya’s mission. At that time all of Māyā’s tricks in the form of insults and dishonor from others here in this country will become insignificant, and we can continue to distribute unlimited books, preach, and spread this movement with purity and in full cooperation with one another, following your order.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, O ocean of mercy, forever bestow your mercy upon us and continue to accept us under your wings, the shelter known as ISKCON.

Your servants in Saltillo, Mexico,  
(written by Hare Kṛṣṇa dāsa)

Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Gaurāṅga-śakti dāsa / Hare Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Bhakta Fernando / Madhusūdana-devī dāsī

Congregation

Kruvadi Satya Narayana / Kanta Kurvadi / Maria Elizabeth

## San Diego

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our sincere, humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet on this most auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your appearance in this material world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have magically manifested Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s spiritual abode and pastimes to the whole world in a most wonderful way. The world is gasping in amazement at your accomplishments: so many devotees, books, temples, restaurants, farms, and many other projects that are blossoming like so many fragrant lotus flowers amidst the thick mire of this Kali-yuga. The miracles that you are performing are truly a manifestation of your love and devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master and your unlimited compassion for the suffering living entities of this world.

You have taught us and shown us that the essence of love is service. And yet, in our neophyte stage of devotion we cannot serve the Lord directly, we cannot love the Lord directly. We can only love the Lord by loving our spiritual master, and we love our spiritual master by serving his mission. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we serve your mission by serving every single manifestation of the spiritual world that you have so mercifully entrusted to us.

We serve Their Lordships in Their Deity forms by remembering Them in everything that we do and offering everything that we have to Them. We serve the holy name of the Lord by chanting very attentive rounds and by taking to the streets with *harināma-saṅkīrtana*. We serve your books, which you labored so intensely to produce and in which Lord Kṛṣṇa and his entire entourage are manifesting themselves, by reading them deeply every day and distributing them in loving ways in ever increasing quantities. We serve the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is so perfectly explained in your books, by deeply understanding it in newer and fresher lights and then teaching it to others to the best of our ability. We serve your temples by keeping them clean from top to bottom and inviting people to come and visit. We serve the holy *dhāmas* by following your instructions to visit them regularly and utilizing our valuable time there to absorb ourselves in *śravaṇam* and *kīrtanam*.

We serve the Vaiṣṇavas that you have created in any way that we can, and especially by discussing topics of Kṛṣṇa and enlivening one another in devotional service. We serve those who have strayed from devotional service by following your instructions ourselves and then, as humbly as we can, begging them to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness to whatever degree they are able. We serve our congregations by visiting their homes and talking heart to heart about Kṛṣṇa and devotional service, and by involving them in your wonderful projects to spread love of Kṛṣṇa around the world.

We serve everyone who walks in the doors of the temple by exhibiting Vaiṣṇava qualities and giving each person such a nice taste of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that they want to come back again and again. We serve everyone we meet, whether on *saṅkīrtana* or at work or in everyday dealings, by attracting them to the sublime message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and encouraging them to visit the temple. We serve all of humanity, using the vision you gave us to see everyone as part and parcel of Kṛṣṇa, by attracting them to the many aspects of this sublime Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We serve all living entities embodied in a myriad of coverings by constantly chanting the holy name everywhere and by offering our food to Kṛṣṇa in devotion. And we serve mother earth by living simply according to Kṛṣṇa’s plan and by teaching the principles of simplicity, truthfulness, and nonviolence to everyone we meet.

In essence, we serve your mission with every ounce of energy and intelligence that we have.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have lifted us from a very hum-drum, mundane, miserable existence and made us pioneers in the most wonderful, exciting spiritual revival that will ever be recorded in history. Despite our many disqualifications, your movement grows and expands by your mercy in ways no one ever thought possible. As your movement is growing, so also are your devotees maturing in their own spiritual lives and in their understanding of your vision for spreading this sublime Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. With the paltry supply of humility and sincerity that is in us, we fervently pray to you to please bless us with the opportunity of serving your mission in a cooperative spirit. And as we absorb ourselves in this spirit of service to you and everything you have given us, please request Lord Kṛṣṇa to manifest His spiritual abode more and more in the hearts of all the world’s inhabitants. Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

*Your insignificant servants in New Govardhana, San Diego, California,*

## Santo Domingo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

What can I do?  
I lack the intelligence,  
the wisdom, and understanding  
to fully express my gratitude  
for what you have done  
for undeserving souls,  
whose only qualification  
is having met you,  
whose only wisdom  
is repeating you,  
whose only job  
is to distribute you,  
and whose only love  
is to please you.

So on this day  
of your worship,  
we pray for your mercy,  
for wisdom to praise you  
not just today  
but every day,  
so that through you  
we may please  
your Lordships,  
Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Your servants in Santo Domingo

## San Paulo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Today is the most auspicious day of the cosmic manifestation, the appearance day of our beloved Guru Mahārāja, His Divine Grace A .C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the ocean of mercy and friend of the fallen, comes personally in every age to reestablish the real *dharma* for the *jīvas,* and He sends His personal associates during the ages in order to give transcendental inspiration for the fallen *jīvas*.

Nowadays the world is merged in sin, due to the effects of Kali-yuga, but although life is becoming more difficult every day, we have the great advantage of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s advent. Lord Caitanya came to give the most valuable and hidden treasure, which is *kṛṣṇa-prema,* a loving relationship with Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in the transcendental land of Vraja.

In the last century, Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement was practically lost, but it was revived by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, one of the confidential servants of Lord Caitanya in the line of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhupāda. The Ṭhākura had an intense desire to propagate the pure *saṅkīrtana* movement through the whole world, and he was the pioneer in preaching to the sinful countries. He had predicted that a great personality would come in the future to propagate Lord Caitanya’s movement throughout the whole world. Now we can see that his prediction became reality and that that great personality is Śrīla Prabhupāda, the dedicated servant of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, who is delivering the sinful countries from the clutches of *nirviśeṣa* and *śūnyavādi*. Śrīla Prabhupāda was born in the same year that Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura sent the first Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava book to the Western countries. In the first meeting with his spiritual master he was instructed to preach Lord Caitanya’s teachings all over the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda is a Rūpānuga, a confidential servant of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, and he is considered by his sincere Godbrothers to be an empowered preacher of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. He is an unlimited ocean of Vaiṣṇava qualities such as humility, compassion, and kindness. He came to deliver the most sinful persons—persons like us, the killers of the cows—and by his devotional power (*bhakti-śakti*) he enables us to feel the cooling rays of the nectarean moon of Śrī Caitanyacandra. Day by day he becomes closer to us through his transcendental and most sublime literature and his sincere disciples. As Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura writes,

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
When thou art living still in sound.  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!

Now let the candid man that seeks to live  
Follow thy way on shores of time,  
Then posterity sure to him will give  
Like one song in simple rhyme!

Let us take shelter at Śrīla Prabhupāda’s holy lotus feet and go back home, back to Godhead, by his divine grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in San Paulo, Brazil,  
(written by Viśva-vandya dāsa)

## Scotland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga. All glories to your International Society for Krishna Consciousness. All glories to the distribution of your transcendental literature.

Please forgive this attempt to glorify you, which is devoid of any real appreciation for what you have actually given us. We owe our whole existence to you, for you have given every aspect of it.

As we go on in your Society and attempt to follow some of the many instructions you gave us, we see just how powerful you are to be able to accomplish so much in such a short space of time. So many books, so many letters, so many conversations, tapes, lectures, videos, photographs, Deities, projects, standards—one easily forgets you are no longer physically present. You planted ISKCON like the seed of a banyan tree, and now we are seeing it growing and giving shelter to the whole planet, spreading into newer areas every day, its roots penetrating even the most barren, godless regions.

Certainly your ISKCON society is no ordinary association. Rather, it is the ongoing manifestation of Lord Caitanya’s *līlā,* allowing us to participate in it and realize Lord Gaurāṅga’s presence. Your activities perfectly mirror the pastimes of Lord Caitanya and His devotees. In Text 163 of the Fifteenth Chapter of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta’s Mādhya-līlā,* entitled “Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya Offers *Prasāda* to the Lord,” the great devotee Śrīla Vāsudeva Datta offers this wonderful prayer to Lord Caitanya. “My dear Lord, let me suffer perpetually in a hellish condition, accepting all the sinful reactions of all living entities. Please finish their diseased material life.” Then in the purport you make the following points:

A Vaiṣṇava is so liberal that he is prepared to risk everything to rescue conditioned souls from material existence.… Śrīla Vāsudeva Datta wanted to completely relieve conditioned souls from material existence so that they would no longer have an opportunity to commit sinful acts.… Nor should one consider Vāsudeva Datta a mundane philanthropist or welfare worker. He was not interested in merging into the Brahman effulgence, nor was he interested in material honor or reputation.… He was a most exalted personality who wanted to show mercy upon conditioned souls.… He was a Vaiṣṇava—*para-duḥkha-duḥkhī—*very much aggrieved to see others suffer. The entire world is purified simply by the appearance of such a great devotee. By his presence the whole world is glorified, and all conditioned souls—due to his transcendental presence—are also glorified. As Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura confirms, Vāsudeva Datta is the ideal devotee of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu:

*gaurāṅgera saṅgi-gaṇe, nitya-siddha kari’ māne,*  
*se yāya vrajendrasuta-pāśa*

**“One who executes Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu’s mission must be considered to be eternally liberated. He is a transcendental person and does not belong to this material world. Such a devotee engaging in the deliverance of the total population is as magnanimous as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself.”**

Just as with Śrīla Vāsudeva Datta, your transcendental presence has made the whole world and the conditioned souls upon it glorious. Each day so many more are chanting the holy name, hearing the Lord’s pastimes, ending their sinful activities by coming in contact with your ISKCON movement. Just one word or even one touch of your books and their lives are changed.

All we need is the mercy of your surrendered servants, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for they have already received your mercy.

**“Whosever welfare you desire immediately becomes a Vaiṣṇava, and Kṛṣṇa delivers all Vaiṣṇavas from the reactions to their past sinful activities” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Mādhya-līlā*** **15.169).**

Now we simply have to execute your instructions faithfully, and all success is guaranteed. “Because of your honest desire, all living entities within the universe will be delivered, for Kṛṣṇa does not have to do anything to deliver all the living entities of the universe” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta Mādhya-līlā* 15.171). And in your purport to Text 179 you write, “One may conclude that according to the sweet will of the devotee, that material world exists or does not exist. Ultimately, however, it is not the sweet will of the devotee but the will of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who, if He so desires, can completely annihilate the material creation.”

Ultimately everything comes down to the will of Kṛṣṇa, and we feel confident that what will endear us to Him most is complying completely with the sweet will of His pure devotee, Your Divine Grace.

Please allow us all to work together more co-operatively, remaining closely aligned like a bunch of sticks. Only when separated can they be broken. Let us give up our own petty independent desires and work as one body to try to please you. You have given us many instructions, and we are attempting to follow just a few. But we hope that by our distributing masses of your books everywhere, the groundwork has been laid for your other programs to blossom.

All glories to you, and all glories to those who are pleasing you. Let us take full shelter of them, for they are eternally liberated souls.

Aspiring to attempt to assist you in your mission, we remain,  
Your devotees in Scotland.

## Seattle

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet, which are the only shelter in the three worlds from the blazing forest fire of material existence. I sincerely pray to be always working hard in your divine service, for that is the only pleasure of my life. Please give me the words to properly glorify you on this holiest of days, your Vyāsa-pūjā.

When I studied different philosophies of religion in college, it was never an accepted fact that a bona fide saintly person still walked the face of the earth. When I first saw you in Los Angeles, my speculative theories and false ideologies were smashed.

I joined your movement in the summer of 1969 in Los Angeles. The devotees in L.A. were always talking about their spiritual leader. At that time I thought your name was Pra-buddha.

I remember when you came to the temple in L.A. and paid your *daṇḍavats* to Lord Jagannātha and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. I was immediately impressed by your humility.

I remember standing outside your door and peeking through the keyhole with Viṣṇujana dāsa.

After services in the temple you would come outside, and all the devotees would be on their knees waiting for you to touch them on the head.

I can still feel you placing your lotus hand on my head. I thought that I had never felt such ecstasy in my life.

I had been praying to Lord Kṛṣṇa that if it were possible, I wanted to render you some personal service. I had been in charge of temples in Phoenix, Tucson, Seattle, and Laguna Beach. In 1975 I was in charge of the Hawaii temple, and I longed to do more personal service for you.

Your servant, Hari-śauri dāsa, came to my office and told me you wanted a cook.

I asked why.

He said you said I was now going to cook your meals.

Kṛṣṇa had made a transcendental arrangement for me to take over the cooking duties for you, and I relish that month I was able to serve you personally.

I prayed to Kṛṣṇa for you to give me some personal words to live by, and Kṛṣṇa made that arrangement also.

I was driving you back to the temple one day and asked you, “Prabhupāda, you said that when a tree has flowers it becomes beautiful, and when a woman has a child she becomes beautiful. When does the disciple become beautiful?”

Because he knew how puffed up I was, Śrīla Prabhupāda immediately chastised me: “Oh, you have not read our books? What is the meaning of this verse, *ār nā koriho mane āśā*?”

“I don’t know, Śrīla Prabhupāda.”

“Hmm … parrotlike chanting.”

I felt like jumping out of the car in embarrassment, but it would have been difficult because I was driving.

Prabhupāda, you were so merciful to me that you allowed me to save face.

“What is the meaning of this: *yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*?”

Excitedly I said, “When we get the mercy of the spiritual master, we get the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Without your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I can’t make any spiritual advancement.”

“Yes, when the disciple is strictly following the orders of the spiritual master, then he is beautiful. Otherwise, he is ugly.”

Prabhupāda, your words burned my heart because I could instantaneously realize how contaminated my heart was.

Your words and instructions are my life and soul, and I am again praying to Kṛṣṇa to burn away any and all desires except one: to be continually engaged in working hard with body, mind, and soul in your transcendental loving service.

Please use your transcendental feet to kick me in any way you wish so that I can stay strongly in your divine service, for that is the only pleasure, the only treasure, and that is the only beauty in this material world.

Your servants in Seattle, Washington,  
(written by Śukadeva dāsa)

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your great self. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Many a time you have stressed that without the mercy of a pure devotee it is impossible for one to make any spiritual advancement. The more we take shelter of your kind, soothing lotus feet, the more we realize the truth of that statement. Without taking shelter of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that there is absolutely no chance of our advancing.

The only way one can be sure of receiving your mercy is to somehow or other be engaged in your transcendental service. You have laid down many plans for increasing the transcendental activities of this great movement, Lord Caitanya’s movement. So, only by your mercy can a sincere devotee attain the desire to help you execute those plans.

We are therefore humbly praying for your mercy so that we will continue trying to fulfill your transcendental desires life after life. We want you to be our *prāṇa-nātha,* the lord of our life. Please help us.

Your insignificant servants in Singapore.

## St. Louis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet! All glories to your divine mission, ISKCON!

As we approach the the fifteenth year after you physically left our presence, we are increasingly feeling your presence through your divine instructions. As we are able to study the instructions you imparted, we feel more and more that you are factually with us in instruction. Of course, this depends on the extent that we are able to follow those instructions. As you said in a room conversation at New Vrindaban on June 23, 1976,

**Prabhupāda:** Now questions?  
**Devotee (1):** So is there ever, for someone whose determination wavers and slackens here and there, is there ever a point where the neophyte devotee is in danger of just forgetting everything and falling, tumbling completely back?  
**Prabhupāda:** Everyone is neophyte. He should practice determination, that’s all. If he cannot practice, then why should he enter into this association? Let him remain aloof. One who has entered with the determination that “I must practice,” so if he cannot practice, then why this makeshow that “I belong to Krsna consciousness movement. I am initiated.” Why this farce? He must practice with determination that “By practicing I’ll be success.” That is wanted. He has no determination, why should he make a show? *Ḍṛḍha-vrata. Bhajante māṁ dṛḍha-vratāḥ. Dṛḍha-vrata,* that is wanted, determination. Hmm, go on. When one is determined, his success is assured. If he’s not determined, then success or failure.  
**Devotee (1):** Can one develop determination gradually?  
**Prabhupāda:** Why gradually? When you promise before your spiritual master that no illicit sex, no gambling, no meat-eating, why should you fall down? If you have no determination, why should you promise in presence of the Deity, fire, spiritual master, Vaiṣṇavas? Why do you make this farce? If you have no determination? If you want to make it a farce, that depends on you. But you should not.  
**Devotee (1):** When we make that promise …  
**Prabhupāda:** Yes, you should not fall down, that is determination. That is gentleman’s determination, that “I have given my promise. Why shall I fall down?” That is determination. “I must respect promise.” That is called *dṛḍha-vrata.* So he’ll succeed. Where is the difficulty? There is no difficulty. But if we want to cheat, that is another thing. If we have no determination, we should not take up this life. Therefore, chance is given that “Stay with us for six months or one year, be determined. Then be initiated.” If you are not determined, what is the use of false initiation?  
**Devotee (1):** Sometimes this weakness …  
**Prabhupāda:** Weakness there, you should rectify weakness. Why you should give any importance to weakness? Weakness is weakness. Rectify it … Initiation means minimum, just beginning …

We are praying to help carry on the *paramparā*. We must, for there is no one else outside of your teachings. That is why you allowed us to call you Prabhupāda. This was not just some sentiment. And the book distribution, Deity worship, *prasādam* distribution, and *harināma* are the weapons we need to take up the battle of preaching. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us part of your mission!

Your servants in St. Louis, Missouri.

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

As the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, in form of Time, frustrates the feverish attempts of the nondevotees. He simultaneously blesses His devotees with the timeless knowledge of the *Vedas.* Śrī Kṛṣṇa sent us you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and you gave us Kṛṣṇa. As the materialistic society heads for ever new dimensions of degradation with all its bizarre “achievements,” your instructions are like a cooling breeze upon the lusty hot minds of the conditioned souls. As one reads your books, sanity, soberness, and honesty immediately start to manifest in the heart of the reader. Your voice, sounding through the pages, brings us back to our original senses, to our original consciousness. Feeling oneself again to be in the reality is a very wonderful realization. As materialists “advance” to hell, your devotees are returning back home, back to Godhead, regaining their original identity.

It is wonderful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are always the same, with all the same unlimited varieties of personal expressions, moods, ecstasies. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like the ocean—deep, vast, pleasing, and grave. As the ocean never changes, neither do you. And as we are carried on the waves of your devotional ecstasies, sometimes finding ourselves in new services, new places, new circumstances, we may ultimately all find absolute peace at your lotus feet, which are the only solid and reliable place in this world. Out of the confusion of this horrible age, you are emerging like a second Mahārāja Dhruva, who simply by the sound of his bow made the frightening illusions of the Yakṣas disappear. One single instruction from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, immediately lights up the darkness we are drowning in.

And so, please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us never forget these instructions you gave us. Don’t let time cover our memory, don’t let sentimentality water down the pure meaning of your words. Don’t let us become “the disciples of the seventies,” or eighties, or nineties. Let us be eternally the same, always following your lotus feet, taking refuge in their shade. Let us take full shelter under the roof you built for us, a roof made from very durable and strong programs like book publication, book distribution, preaching, the Sunday feast, *prasādam* distribution, and *harināma saṅkīrtana*. This roof is so strong, it will resist all the storms of Kali-yuga. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this strongest of all shelters. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being always the same, never-changing ultimate shelter of all conditioned souls. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at the Hare Krishna Center, Govinda’s Restaurant, Stockholm, Sweden,

Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Maṇidhara dāsa

Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

## Sumi

I offer my respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

It is difficult to express our gratitude to Śrīla Prabhupāda for taking up the mission of saving people from this material world, which was blessed by the touch of his lotus feet. People in general are not able to appreciate the activities of great souls; that is why *mahātmās* are compassionate and tolerant in their preaching mission as they propagate the philosophy of the ancient Vedic scriptures, which can save one from the miseries of material life. Lord Kṛṣṇa does not want the living entities to suffer in this miserable material world, and so He sends His servants and His associates here to teach the conditioned souls Kṛṣṇa consciousness and enable them to return back home, back to Godhead. All this takes place by the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

I should like again and again to express gratitude on behalf of the people of my town, who might have suffered in this confused material world for many lifetimes had Śrīla Prabhupāda not illuminated the path to the spiritual world. Our only duty now is to endeavor to follow this path under the guidance of ISKCON’s authoritative preachers and spiritual masters, Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciples. On this very auspicious day, I, a conditioned soul, ask Śrīla Prabhupāda to please bless me so I may participate in his mission and serve him.

All glories to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The devotees in Sumi, the Ukraine, USSR,  
(written by Bhakta Vladmir)

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance.

We the devotees at your Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in Sydney, Australia, are eternally indebted to Your Divine Grace for your special benediction upon us. By giving us the opportunity to participate in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement and to serve Their extremely merciful Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, we are receiving the benediction of your extraordinary prayer to Kṛṣṇa, “Dear Rādhā-Gopīnātha, now I am leaving You in the hands of the *mlecchas*. I cannot take the responsibility. You please guide these boys and girls and give them the intelligence to worship You very nicely.”

Now, exactly twenty years later, Their Lordships are resplendently situated on a golden throne as the sole proprietors of Their own temple and are surrounded by many jewels—recipients of their mercy: *mlecchas* turned into jewels of devotees. The Deity worship is going on in great opulence, and with many flowers decorating the altar. Hundreds of conditioned souls are visiting the temple every day to receive *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. Every day our restaurant is packed, and many more people receive *prasādam* through the Hare Kṛṣṇa Food for Life program, also based in the temple. In fact, just recently we were able to distribute our two millionth free meal. This year plans are under way to increase the temple book distribution to a respectable level, which will be more pleasing to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please help us to be more fully engaged in your service and give us the ability to continue to maintain what you have given us. On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, let us rededicate our lives to your mission. At this time let us meditate on the divine instructions you have implanted in our hearts and strive to realize them in the year ahead.

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to pray to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha to give us all the intelligence to nicely serve you and Their Lordships eternally.

Your servants in Sydney, Australia,

## Tallahassee

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

lease accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga.

The disciples should feel like dog puppies before their spiritual master. You taught us the glorious process of *saṅkīrtana-yajña,* by which true humility and all the other godly qualities could be easily developed. You yourself are the best example—perfect from all angles. You patiently encouraged us and asked us to learn to love *saṅkīrtana*. Furthermore, you explained that book distribution was not only one of the devotional practices but also the fastest way to obtain Lord Caitanya’s mercy, as well as what gave you the highest pleasure. Some of us must have been thinking of something else while you spoke, and we ended up barking up the wrong tree.

We have been very meager in our efforts to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but if we have anything of value to offer to your divine lotus feet, it is that this temple has been exclusively maintained by book distribution.

Please grant us a place at your lotus feet.

Your insignificant servants in Tallahassee, Florida,

## Tampa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances.

For the past two years and five months I have been maintaining your ISKCON Temple here in Tampa, Florida. On the request of your GBC representative, His Holiness Hridayānanda Swami, I came here to maintain this center. We have a large house and a big temple room. We are located half a mile from Interstate Highway 275 and one block from a main six-lane street. We are also right next to a very peaceful ten-acre city park.

The presiding Deities here are expansions of Rādhā-Vṛndāvanacandra, your beloved Deities in New Vrndavana. They were created from molds as replicas and are just as beautiful.

I support the temple 95% myself by book distribution locally, and I get help from members. Every week we receive many guests who come to see Kṛṣṇa, bow down, chant, and take *prasādam*. Because you are an exalted, bona fide representative of Kṛṣṇa, our programs, which you have instructed us to execute, are changing people’s lives and uplifting them from the darkest ignorance to eternal bliss.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, deserve all the credit. I have no qualifications. I am simply trying to carry out your orders to distribute your books, worship the Deities, and preach *Bhagavad-gītā*. It is your amazing grace and kindness that you have saved my life.

I was like a wounded, bleeding dog lying in the street hit by a car, heaving and dying a slow, miserable death. You so gently and kindly lifted me up and carried me despite my smelly, doggish existence. Carefully, patiently you revived me from my wounded stupor of ignorance. You have brought me all the way to the ever-blissful shining lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and I am eternally indebted to you for this.

Let me offer my life and service to you. I am living on your potency only; without you I am lost.

Your servants in Tampa, Florida,  
(written by Vivasvān dāsa)

Vivasvān dāsa / Cakradhara dāsa / Bhakta Matt / Bhaktin Lisa

## Tashkent

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most humble and unlimited obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious day of your appearance.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*

We devotees of the Tashkent *yātrā* offer you our deep respect. We are trying to continue Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* mission in Middle Asia in order to save the fallen souls from hell. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only by your causeless mercy and the mercy of Lord Kṛṣṇa that we have received the transcendental knowledge from your books and have the opportunity to distribute this knowledge to others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us distribute more and more of your wonderful books—as much as possible.

Your servants in Tashkent, USSR.

## Tbilisi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances.

I live in Tbilisi, USSR, and by your mercy have the opportunity to chant sixteen rounds, read your books, and serve Gaura-Nitāi.

You are an ideal example of service to the spiritual master.

I don’t know what to do for you and for your mission. I have no sincerity, no devotion, and no intelligence, nor do I possess the six good qualities. I simply search for peace and happiness. I am so blind that when I attain a little peace and happiness, I forget Kṛṣṇa. Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura says that while the demons killed by Kṛṣṇa become free, he will remain here. Then what to speak of me?

Your disciples say that the grandfather-*guru* is more merciful than the father-*guru*. That’s why I beg for your causeless mercy, so that I can attain a drop from the ocean of Gaura-Nitāi’s and the *guru-paramparā’s* mercy. I beg never to forget the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* to understand that Kṛṣṇa and His name are nondifferent, that you are always present in your books, and that I am the eternal servant of my *guru* and Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The servants of your servant in Tbilisi, USSR.  
(written by Govindānanda dāsa)

## Tokyo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world, let us offer our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement!

Most of the devotees here never had your personal association, but by the mercy of your disciples, the Japanese devotees here are engaged in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you very much for your transcendental *vāṇī* association.

Here in Japan there is so much influence of impersonalism and voidism. Your books are well received here in Japan. Not only the devotees but Japanese *karmīs* also love the Japanese *Kṛṣṇa* book, which was just published last year. Thank you very much for all these transcendental books, which is your offenseless chanting. By reading and distributing these books, we are able to chant the glories of the Lord without offense.

The advancement of Japanese technology simply leads people to hell unless we use it in the service of the Lord. In *The Nectar of Devotion,* you quote Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī as follows: “Actual auspiciousness means welfare activities for all the people of the world.” Your *saṅkīrtana* movement is the highest welfare activity. So Japanese devotees are responsible to engage Japanese cars and computers, etc., in the service of the Lord.

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg your mercy so that we can stay in your service life after life.

Your servants in Japan,

Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda
