## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

As we pay our respectful obeisances daily and on this Vyāsa-pūjā Day of 1991, we glorify you as *gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe,* practising and preaching the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Indeed, as you come in the disciplic succession from Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, it is by your divine grace only that we know anything of that all-attractive Supreme Person. Your lotus feet are the only gateway to Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes (*śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei sei uttama gati*), and your service is inseparable from His (*yathā deve tathā gurau*). You are the dearest servitor of Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth because you are eternally, consciously, and blissfully surrendered to be solely an instrument of Lord Gaurāṅga to spread *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* and its culture all over the world (*gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*).

To attain the essence of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* love of Godhead (*premā pum-artho mahān*), Caitanya Mahāprabhu especially recommends five activities of devotional service, and you are the exemplar and propagator of all of them.

1\. **Chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa:** You teach us that the name Kṛṣṇa is the person Kṛṣṇa; there is no difference. Because you chant Hare Kṛṣṇa without offense, you are ever absorbed in love of Kṛṣṇa. Because you are humble, you are surrendered to the holy name. “When the holy name is chanted by a pure devotee of the Lord in love, it has the greatest efficacy on hearers.” The explosive result of your preaching is proof of this. Your appearance in the park in New York City in 1966 chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* might have looked quaint yet insignificant to the mundaners of that matrix of materialism, but your sound vibration was descending from the spiritual world, penetrating through the heart to the deepest self, manifesting the transformative power of spiritual awakening. In the field of the world, you truly “came in like a needle, went out like a plow.” As the seed-giving father, you continue to turn furrows and plant seeds in that field, where devotional creepers continue to grow, nourished by the rays of the rising full moon. By watering daily, as you instructed, may we come to drink the sweet nectar of the holy name of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

2\. **Hearing Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:** *Śrīmad-bhāgavatam pramāṇam amalam.* It is the opinion of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu that *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is the spotless Vedic literature, the literary incarnation of God, and it has arisen like the sun to dissipate the darkness of Kali-yuga. Yet we are so merged in that densest of darkness, so far from the standard position, that it is impossible to know or even hear the book *Bhāgavatam* in practice. Your spiritual master ordered you to preach Kṛṣṇa’s eternal message (westward) in English and said, “If you ever get money, print books.” You made his instructions your life and soul and attempted to fulfill them, whether anyone helped or not, whether results manifested or not, and so Caitanya Mahāprabhu blessed your service with those results, because you are that person *bhāgavata* (*gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*). By hearing these two *bhāgavatas* always (*nityaṁ bhāgavata-sevayā*), may we attain to the devotional service of the holy name.

3\. **Associating with Devotees:** As you stressed many times, this principle is so important that it comes before or with the others: *ādau śraddhā tataḥ sādhu-saṅgo ‘tha bhajana-kriyā.…* How can we, whose association before your divine appearance was overwhelmingly with the three modes of material nature, know to perform the others without the association of a *sādhu* whose very life is the practice and preaching of these *bhakti* principles? The bound are freed by the unbound, and Kṛṣṇa is found by the association of devotees, which alone can place one in devotional service. You mercifully saved us by giving us your association, but you did more. Like the seed of a growing crystal, as the founder-*ācārya* you are the *bījam avyayam* of a society within which we can gain the association of devotees. “Anyone seriously interested in deriving benefit by studying the *Bhagavad-gītā* must take help from the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement for practical understanding of *Bhagavad-gītā* under the direct guidance of the Lord.” May we always associate with the most intelligent souls who worship Lord Caitanya with His associates by chanting the holy name with the assembled devotees.

4\. **Worshiping the Deities:** According to *Bhagavad-gītā*, we were all born into delusion, bewildered by dualities arisen from desire and hate, in association with a body that is temporary, ignorant, and miserable. But the Lord is so kind that we can experience the eternal Absolute, gain inspiration and enlightenment, and engladden ourselves in this world by tasting transcendental happiness simply by seeing Him (*darśana*) as the Deity form. Even as neophytes in darkness, we can behold Lord Kṛṣṇa, the source of all light, and realize our relationship with Him. But this is possible only because you, as Kṛṣṇa’s pure and great devotee, have requested Him to manifest His Deity form (*ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīno hy asvatantra iva dvija*). You know Śrī Kṛṣṇa most intimately, and you have given Him to us so that we may become like you. By seeing the Deity daily, may we chant the holy name without offense.

5\. **Living in Vṛndāvana:** *Ārādhyo bhagavān vrajeśa-tanayas tad-dhāma vṛndāvanam.* It is the opinion of Lord Caitanya that just as the son of Nanda is supremely worshipable, so His abode, Vṛndāvana, which is nondifferent from Him and which He never leaves, is similarly worshipable. You demonstrated this by your example during your time of preparation when you lived in Vṛndāvana, taking inspiration from Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Jīva Gosvāmī at the holy place of their *bhajana* and *samādhi.* But you did more than that. Apparently leaving Vṛndāvana by the order of Kṛṣṇa and *guru,* you took Vṛndāvana with you because Kṛṣṇa went with you by His order and by your pure *bhakti* of eternal remembrance of Him. “Wherever You are, O Lord, *there* is Vṛndāvana.” You expanded Vṛndāvana (*ānandāmbudhi-vardhanam*) so that we could live in or visit Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s abode (*sarvātma-snapanam*). By your divine mercy, may we chant the holy name and serve Vṛndāvana.

As we offer our obeisances to you as *gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe,* these five ways that you established by example will empower us to serve your lotus feet. Only by service to your lotus feet can we become free from pride and hypocrisy by gaining the true humility born of knowledge of our constitutional position as eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa, and giving birth to constant surrender to the holy name.

Your aspiring servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma, Toronto, Canada.

## Tucson

Descended from the spiritual sky,  
Upon Kṛṣṇa’s promise did you rely:  
“Just preach My message to all you meet;  
With faith in Me, you’ll face no defeat.”

Taken birth in the land of piety,  
Earliest memories of your father’s Deity,  
Performing Ratha-yātrā, your sister by your side,  
Foretold in the śāstra—everything did coincide.

While you pursued your studies, Gandhi caught your eye,  
India’s independence—the British system to decry.  
But then one day, by an insistent mate,  
You met your Guru Mahārāja, as ordained by fate.

“You are educated young men, so kindly preach this cult;  
Lord Caitanya’s movement is pure and without any fault.  
It will benefit you and all whom you meet;  
So bring all souls to the shelter of His lotus feet.”

You took to heart the words of that great saint,  
With full faith and conviction,  
showing no restraint.  
Preaching to the lowest the message so sublime,  
Degenerated youth and Vedic culture to align.

No one can comprehend your supreme ability,  
And all was accomplished with the greatest humility.  
All who got your blessings were released from their karma,  
And now so many are on the path of sanātana-dharma.

You set the perfect example—to work in full devotion.  
We need to get back into the swing of that motion.  
Pioneering for you, to stay in your grace,  
Opening up new temples all over the place!  
It says in the scriptures that the desert is cursed,  
That to perform yajña there is almost the worst,  
But by your divine grace we’re finding tremendous success,  
Receptive souls to preach to in great excess.

We want to offer you a spiritual oasis  
Where harināma and books are the basis,  
And a prasādam restaurant to attract the masses  
To hearing and chanting and Bhāgavatam classes.  
Your movement will expand as long as there is love and trust,  
And as long as we, your disciples, stay free from false ego and lust.  
And your books daily we faithfully read,  
But most important—it’s your causeless mercy we need!

Please keep us in your lotuslike heart forever, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and always engaged in your preaching mission. Our lives are yours.

Your unworthy servants in Tucson, Arizona,  
(written by Sandaminī-devī dāsī)

## Turkmenistan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances from all of us here at the Turkmenistan *yātrā*. We shall distribute your books with great enjoy to all Turkmenistan’s souls.

*Jaya* Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Turkmenistan, USSR.

## Udhampur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus like feet. You are the savior of the whole world, for you are spreading *sanātana-dharma* throughout the whole world.

In *Bhagavad-gītā* Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa says,

*avajānanti māṁ mūḍhā*  
*mānuṣīṁ tanum āśritam*  
*paraṁ bhāvam ajānanto*  
*mama bhūta-maheśvaram*

We were among the *mūḍhas,* so to know about Śrī Kṛṣṇa and relish His devotional service seemed quite impossible for us. Yet it happened—like a dream come true—due to your limitless mercy of giving us shelter under your lotuslike feet. You have given us the opportunity to serve the lotus feet of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the Śrīla Prabhupāda Ashram, under the most merciful guidance of your loving spiritual son Navayogendra Swami. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have enabled us to practice the teachings of *Bhagavad-gītā* in a most easy way by giving us the association of devotees and the highest religious principle, which Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa gave:

*sarva-dharmān parityajya*  
*mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja*  
*ahaṁ tvāṁ sarva-pāpebhyo*  
*mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ*

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will always be indebted to you for this, for we cannot repay you in millions and millions of births.To conclude our humble prayers at the dust of your lotus feet, we beg you to please allow us to serve your lotus feet birth after birth in ISKCON. This is the only way to overcome the effects of the Kali-yuga, and thus whoever takes shelter of this house built by you shall be saved. This is our firm belief.

*śri-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama janāra bandhu,*  
*lokanāth lokera jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhu koro doyā, deho more pada-chāyā,*  
*ebe yaśa ghuṣuk tri-bhuvana*

*dui-pade laile śaraṇa*  
*prabhupāda patita-pāvana*

Your eternal servants in Udhampur, India,

Yadukuleśvara dāsa / Sāmba dāsa / Sudarśana dāsa / Bhakta Sham / Bhakta Daneshwar

## Vienna

**The Sweetest Bliss**

Prabhupāda, who will distribute your books if we do it no more?  
The foolish materialists would never buy them in a store,  
Not hesitating to purchase any other damn thing—  
“Who cares if it kills you or if you call it a sin?”—

Because your books are all about Kṛṣṇa and surrender,  
And that’s the first thing the foolish rascals put for later.  
They’d rather get ruined with drugs or blown up in a war.  
That’s the perverted mentality we’re trying to reform.

But if we go out with sincerity and devotional power,  
We can induce them to take home this nectarean shower.  
In similar straits would we be, Prabhupāda, if you had not come.  
A painful life of sin and ignorance you delivered us from.

If we don’t go out and distribute these books,  
Getting people to take them home to read,  
Or even to just touch them—”At least have a look”—  
How will they ever get a clue to ultimate truth?

The full truth, that is,  
Nothing else comes close.  
And when we really believe that,  
It won’t be just a boast.

Not all of the New Age esoteric  
“Consciousness raising” in the world  
Can do people as much good  
As a single line or even a word  
From any one of your wonderful books!  
And when we really believe that—  
That Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the solution  
To all of life’s complex problems—  
Firm conviction will be our resolution.

Then we’ll make it our business,  
From our determination within,  
To give a book to everyone  
Through thick and thin.

No matter how high or how low,  
Approaching everybody we know,  
We’ll meet people everywhere, like so,  
“Just take some of these before you go!”

But in order to give, we’ve got to believe them,  
And that means that we have got to read them  
And practice the words with enthusiasm robust,  
Or else for certain be overcome by the enemy lust.

O Prabhupāda, I’m praying to you  
Once more in the heart of my heart—  
Please give me the strength and purpose  
To make another start.

With your books in my hand  
And at Mahāprabhu’s command—  
“On My order become guru;  
Now go out and save this land!”

If we just go out and try,  
That’s all that’s required,  
Because the process is based upon desire:  
Surrender fully and you’ll get into the fire.

Then we’ll feel a special bliss  
That’s sweeter than the sweetest kiss!  
I know. I’m sure. (Because I’ve felt it before.)  
The taste of nectar we’re always looking for.

Your servants in Vienna, Austria,

Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Śrīla dāsa

Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

## Vladivostok

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Though we are just taking our first steps in devotional service, we are still able to appreciate the gift you have given us. Now our lives before we took to Kṛṣṇa consciousness seem like a bad dream. We all were covered by the gross illusion of material existence, and only by the grace of your sincere disciples do we now have hope for salvation from this hellish condition.

Exactly twenty years ago you blessed our ill-fated land with the touch of your lotus feet. Since that time many people of our country have joined your movement and became devotees of Kṛṣṇa. It was a very hard time for them, but they were inspired by the transcendental power of your books, and it helped them to distribute this bliss-giving knowledge.

Actually we can’t even realize how fortunate we are that we got the chance to follow this spiritual path. Who can say how many millions of years we were wandering in the darkness of this material world until we received your mercy? You saved our lives, and we feel very much obliged to you. The only way to repay our debt to you is to help you go on spreading this great mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We are not qualified enough to glorify you, so we’re just trying our best to strictly follow all your instructions and distribute your wonderful books in order to attract other fallen souls to Kṛṣṇa.

Again and again we offer our obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy.

Your eternal servants in Vladivostok, USSR.

## Vozonezh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Devotees from around the world write homages to you on this most auspicious day. Every devotee tries to express his or her gratitude for your mercy in establishing and maintaining ISKCON and allowing us to belong to it. ISKCON gives every possibility to conditioned souls to go back home, back to Godhead. This is our real goal, which we forgot during millions and millions of births, in which we were drowning in the ocean of material existence. You gave the possibility to us miserable souls of Kali-yuga to get onboard the ship of transcendental knowledge and cross this ocean under the guidance of one of your disciples, whose instructions we accept as the instructions of God Himself. Devotees distribute your books day after day during the year, they chant sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* every day, listen to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, worship the Deities in the temple, and do other services.

It is very difficult to restrain ourselves from sense gratification, to balance the mind, and to escape Māyā’s tricks, because our intelligence is very small. Thus it is difficult to remember that we are not the supreme controller and enjoyer. Likewise, it is difficult to remember Kṛṣṇa, even if we desire to remember Him. Therefore it is not surprising that many, many people do not want to hear about Kṛṣṇa and live idle lives, wasting their valuable time.

Our actual situation is that at every moment we may find ourselves in the clutches of Māyā because of our false identification with the body. But by your mercy we taste nectarean *prasādam,* lead *kīrtana* before the Deities, study and distribute your books, and associate with Vaiṣṇavas. In order to accept your mercy, bestowed on us through your disciples, we must concentrate on book distribution, be attentive and serious while chanting the holy names, and listen to and preach the *Bhāgavatam’s* message.

Therefore I ask you for blessings in order that I may follow these processes properly. Occasionally I make offenses while serving or chanting, and therefore I ask you to be merciful and forgive me for them. I am trying to become better.

All glories to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission!

Your servants in Vozonezh, USSR.  
(written by Bhakta Aundrey)

## Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

The Lord eternally resides with His most intimate associates in the pleasure groves of Vṛndāvana. Therefore, naturally Vṛndāvana is your home. It is by your unlimited mercy that we have come to realize that we are merely strangers in the land of our birth. We are only tiny parts and parcels of the vast unlimited Lord, and by is kindness we may serve Him in His supreme abode, if we wish for eternity.

Today, sixteen years ago, you returned home to install Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, who are nondifferent from our all-merciful Lords Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Prabhu Nityānanda. Even while performing the first *ārati* after the installation ceremony, you turned to the public while waving the peacock fan and asked them to chant the holy name.

Who else but you could have thought of this?

We were wandering in the dark and ugly Kali-yuga, slaves of our senses, with drugs on every street corner, where people say that without meat one cannot live, where expertise in sex determines how much of a man or woman one is, and money is the only thing people respect. We were hopelessly addicted.

You came from a different world, pure and spiritual, where the Lord appears in a beautiful threefold-bending form, with head bedecked with peacock feather, with lotus eyes, and with unlimited wonderful qualities. How could anyone understand?

You explained how your Godbrothers went to the West and spoke with the Marquis of Zetland, who asked if he could become a *brāhmaṇa*. “Yes, certainly,” was the reply. “All that is required is to give up all illicit sex, meat-eating, intoxication, and gambling.” “Impossible!” the Marquis replied. You told how when you remembered this, you thought to yourself, “Impossible … but let me try anyway!”

You left Vṛndāvana and lived alone among *mlecchas*. But you didn’t mind, for you knew that Lord Kṛṣṇa accompanied you.

You lived on the Bowery in a madman’s loft, and in Haight Ashbury with hippies for an audience, and Hells Angels for neighbors. But you were prepared to live with anyone, and as long as they chanted Hare Kṛṣṇa you were tolerant.

Who of all Vaiṣṇavas would have done such a thing? Who is more merciful than you are?

How expertly you fanned the spark in the heart of those who had the faith to chant. Again and again you transformed Jagāi and Mādhāi with the holy name and your love for them.

Gradually and patiently you introduced more and more knowledge, higher standards, the *arcā-vigraha,* the morning program, and so on.

Today we behold your heritage and pray to be strong. You gave us everything—a way to live, a task to complete, a mission to fulfill. You gave us a vision of what the world should be, and also what our business is while Kali is doing his work of destruction. Yes, it is an emergency, we must extinguish the blazing forest. Please give us determination and conviction so that we may push on to bring about the revolution in the impious lives of a misdirected civilization. There are so many weaknesses within our hearts; attachments from so long are pushing so strong.

But we have faith that for those who really try to chant their rounds, and who cling to your program, your books, your temples, and service to you as the rope to save their lives, there is no question that old habits will ever pull them down.

Today we are in Vṛndāvana and find ourselves amazed at how kind you have been to bring us here. As we relish the *dhāma* and never wish to leave again, we just begin to realize that as yet the full potency of the *dhāma* has not even begun to manifest for us.

Some think there is more to taste than you have given. I become sad when I hear them speak this way and pray that they may return to the position of staunchly following only you. This will allow us to reach the Lord’s lotus feet in the spiritual world without a doubt.

They come to Vṛndāvana and other *dhāmas* and shop outside, but in the name of the “real inner thing” they buy only doubt. Yes, there are other great Vaiṣṇavas present on the planet, and from them there certainly is a lot to learn. But of all Vaiṣṇavas you were the one who lived with us and knew what we are really worth. Let us sweep the temple, let us study and distribute your books, and let us preach this knowledge to whomever wants to hear, just as you did. Let us never waste a moment but always chant the name of the Lord, just as you did. We don’t need to hear the intimate pastimes of Kṛṣṇa and the *gopīs* beyond what you have told us, for there is no doubt that by following you we’ll become qualified one day, and then all will be revealed to us from within the heart.

Some were with you from the very beginning and had their lives transformed. How can they ever forget a single moment with you? Others came later and may have barely met you in person, but they will never forget even one precious moment. Then there are the many devotees who have come after you left this mortal world. But it doesn’t matter, for, touched by your mercy somehow or other, all of us are fortunate.

Today we can only confess that we have fallen short every step of the way. We know there is nothing we have done that makes us qualified, but still we beg your mercy anyway, praying that we may always remain at your lotus feet.

Your servants at the Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira, Vṛndāvana, India,

## Washington, D. C.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your inconceivable blessings upon us.

Your fame is spread all over the three worlds; we take shelter of your lotus feet.

Since your disappearance, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been working hard to fulfill your desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

Your staunch leaders have been like military commanders infiltrating Māyā’s camp, by your mercy.

Despite great difficulties, your BBT has been printing many new books in many languages for the benefit of our fallen society, by your mercy.

Your temples have been regularly distributing many plates of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* by your mercy.

In your temple here in the countryside near Washington, D.C., your first *mūrtis* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana are still being worshiped, although conservatively, by your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, more importantly your transcendental literatures have been distributed to thousands of visitors, by your mercy.

We pray, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to constantly surrender and to continue, despite all obstacles, to spread *sanātana-dharma* all over the world, by your mercy.

Your humble servants in New Hastināpura, Potomac, Maryland,

## Wellington

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā 1991. We, your aspiring humble and insignificant grandchildren here at the ISKCON temple in Wellington, reflect on the contribution you made to this world. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you saved us from unlimited suffering! You gave us transcendental knowledge to destroy our ignorance! You are so merciful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you sacrificed your nights: when all the world was sleeping, you were preaching to the human race by translating your wonderful works—*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and *Bhagavad-gītā*. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, who knows your glories? Your books will preach to the world for ten thousand years. Your *bṛhat mṛdaṅga* will sound far and wide! Because you desire it, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Kṛṣṇa will use us, your servants, as instruments.

How insignificant our position is next to yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda—like pebbles next to the Himalayas! You taught us how to wash properly, how to eat, and what to eat. Everything we do in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is an expansion of your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

I very humbly pray that we, your aspiring servants here at the Wellington temple, may always be very faithful and surrendered grandchildren situated at your lotus feet! Although we feel most unqualified to represent you, we are also hoping against hope that we may qualify to receive full shelter at your lotus feet, one-hundred-percent surrendered. You saved us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so we are all eternally indebted to you. So this year we will all preach to the best of our capacity, as you so much desired. Desiring your mercy every moment, we, your servants in the Wellington *yātrā,* offer our most humble obeisances to your lotus feet millions of times.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! May your glories be spread throughout the three worlds.

Your servants in Wellington, New Zealand,

Rohiṇī-nandana dasa / Rāmacandra dasa / Sītārāṇī dasi

Congregation

Bhakta Stuart / Bhakta Marcus / Raja Ram and family / Jagdish / Mohad / Moha / Raja and Sashi / Sineh Prem

## Yogyakarta

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this anniversary of your most auspicious appearance for the deliverance of so many fallen conditioned souls, we beg to offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet and some simple words in your glorification.

Your birth and activities are all *divya,* transcendental, just like those of Lord Kṛṣṇa in His many incarnations. You are Lord Kṛṣṇa’s em-powered representative in this world. Your every thought, word, and action establishes the path of pure devotional service. Through your transcendental literatures you continue to touch the hearts of millions and reclaim them back to Godhead. You are the transparent via medium through whom the mercy of all the *ācāryas* in the *guru-paramparā,* the six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, Śrī Pañca-tattva, and all their associates, right up to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and all Their intimate servants, is flowing freely to all those willing to receive it.

Among all of Kṛṣṇa’s servants, you must be the most dear, because no one ever before so widely distributed Kṛṣṇa consciousness, even to the most fallen and sinful persons. You are engaging everyone in four potent spiritual activities of distributing and reading your books, chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* and taking *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. By this practical program, you are showing how any man or woman in any part of the world can make his or her life perfect and go back to Godhead.

Your glories have been nicely described in offerings by devotees from all over the world collected in this book. We are the most insignificant servants of your servants, trying to establish a center to preach your mission in a far-off corner of the world, and our offering may be one of the last in the book. However, we join with thousands of devotees in glorifying you on this day, and we pray for just one drop of your mercy so we may assist you in your glorious mission of giving Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone.

Your insignificant servants in Yogyakarta, Indonesia,

Śubhānanda dāsa / Satāṅgati dāsa / Bhaktin Suyatni / Veda-kusuma

## Zagreb

Dear Grandfather, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances, and our gratitude! We had nothing before you came to the West. You came, and although we were the lowest, you gave us the highest. What gift can compare to this? We had no culture, we had no knowledge, no future, no hope. We were lifeless sacks of chemicals with no goal in life, with no alternative, with no one to follow, love, and trust. We were strangers in this dark world, with no friends around us. We were unhappy and without shelter. We had no good qualities and were faithless atheists with no knowledge of morality, what to speak of spiritual principles. We did not know how to eat or what to eat. We did not know what is good and what is bad, nor how human beings should behave. We were helpless and confused in the whirlpool of *māyā*. In this materialistic society we were drowning in speculations and experiments. The only certainty was eventual death. In this nightmare we were shaking like exhausted street dogs looking up fearfully, begging for shelter and relief. We were at the edge of existence.

And then you came and brought everything with you. You gave us knowledge, eternity, and bliss. You gave us the goal, the path, and the means. You gave us wonderful Lord Kṛṣṇa and His precious holy name. What was already sweet you made even tastier so that even fools like us would accept. From the streets you compassionately picked us up and gave us shelter, a real home—your wonderful ISKCON. From you we got a wonderful family, too: fathers, uncles, brothers, sisters, and relatives. We got our past, present, and future. You gave us civilization. You never offered some suspicious speculations, but crystal-clear instructions. You changed history, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Like Mahārāja Parīkṣit, you chased Kali away. You showed us how one should live a humble but glorious life. Above all, you gave us your most devoted followers.

We had nothing; you gave us everything. To the lowest, you offered the highest. What event can compare to this?

Your granddisciples in Zagreb, Yugoslavia,

## Zurich

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Today is your Vyāsa-pūjā day, the most glorious and wonderful occasion for us, the members of your ISKCON, and actually for the whole world. On this most auspicious day not only a few very fortunate souls but everyone in the world should come together and remember and glorify your transcendental auspicious appearance. Unfortunately, only a relative few understand and appreciate the wonderful activities you performed for the upliftment of the entire humanity. Even among your followers, who understands your real greatness in full? Will it ever by possible for us to completely understand your personality and your transcendental plans? *Vaiṣṇavera kriyā mudrā vijñeha nā bujhāya:* it is very difficult to understand a pure devotee of the Lord. Only other pure devotees can understand a most pure devotee.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that you and your mission will become more and more manifest in our hearts. And please bless us so that we will be allowed to always remain in the association of your unalloyed and intimate servants. What in the world has any value except the association with a pure devotee? As Śrīla Rāmānanda Rāya said, “The only auspicious activity is association with the devotees of Kṛṣṇa.”

*ei kali-kāle āra nāhi kona dharma*  
*vaiṣṇava, vaiṣṇava-śāstra, ei kahe marma*

**“In this age of Kali, there are no genuine religious principles. There are only the Vaiṣṇava devotees and the Vaiṣṇava devotional scriptures. This is the sum and substance of everything” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā,*** **9.362).**

Our only desire is to seek the shelter of your lotus feet and of your instructions in your books, as well as the association of the devotees who endeavor to follow your instructions and who are, therefore, very dear to Your Divine Grace.

Your causeless mercy manifests in the distribution of your books. What else, then, is to be done except to distribute your books? By your mercy, book distribution will always go on.

“Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees. O master! Be merciful unto us. Give us the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds.”

As far as I am concerned, I can only fall at your lotus feet and ask for forgiveness for all the offenses I have committed. Please engage me in the nectarean service to your transcendental mission birth after birth, and please let me always stay in the association of your pure devotees.

In our temple here in Zurich, we are completing a museum in your honor, presenting your glorious activities all over the world. I hope we will be able to open it on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. There will be a big library with all of your books in all the languages of the world. In this way everybody who visits this temple will be aware of your unique achievements.

All glories to you, Mahā-patita-pāvana, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Zurich and other centers in Switzerland,  
(written by Rohiṇī-suta dāsa)

## Homages from the Publishers

## Bombay BBT

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You have delivered us from the ocean of birth and death. A drowning swimmer in the middle of the ocean cannot save himself. Only if he is picked up by a boat can he be saved. Your lotus feet are that boat offering shelter from this ocean of birth and death.

Please bless us so that our efforts will be concentrated only on repaying the debt we owe you. Of course, how could we ever repay you? Impossible! Still we must try.

Your servants at the Bombay branch of the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust,

Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Mahāmantra dāsa / Bhīma dāsa

Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda

Nārada ṛṣi dāsa / Śrīnivāsa Ācārya dāsa / Nandanandana dāsa / Kailāśa-candra dāsa / Lokamānya dāsa / Rāma-avatāra dāsa / Bhakta Narayan / Bhaktin B. M Agarwal

## Indonesian BBT

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupada,

You appeared in this material world to save the fallen souls;  
You reminded them of Kṛṣṇa and taught them life’s true goals.  
Your Guru Mahārāja’s words and Kṛṣṇa you ne’er forgot;  
Thus you’re empowered to save those long condemned in hell to rot.

You imbibed the essence of all śāstras at the ācāryas’ lotus feet.  
Your books’ one line, if one will read—perfection he will meet.  
You boldly preached the message of the Gītā As It is,  
And smashed all speculations on who Kṛṣṇa really is.

You dared to challenge mundane scientists who concoct and cheat and lie,  
“Put Kṛṣṇa in the center or you’re no better than a fly!”  
It’s only by your mercy Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa we can serve.  
Now from the path of devotion we must not ever swerve.

You gave us books, prasādam, taught us how to really live.  
Now that pure message you ordered us to others we must give.  
We beg one drop of mercy to serve you beyond birth and death,  
To go on preaching like you did until our final breath.

Your insignificant servants at the Indonesian BBT,

Gaura-maṇḍala-bhūmi dāsa / Aṅgalatā-devī dāsī / Vyāsa-pūjā dāsa

## Mediterranean BBT

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, the empowered representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead who has come to deliver our poor selves lost in an ocean of delusion and pride.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, countless times you repeated to us that Kṛṣṇa has to be understood through love and devotion, but Māyā tries her best to induce the tiny conditioned souls to believe in the power of their minds, so they may attempt to grasp the limits of the *acintya-śakti,* the inconceivable potency of the Supreme Person.

\* \* \*

**Prabhupāda:** Therefore *acintya.* Therefore *acintya,* inconceivable. Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. Don’t try to understand Kṛṣṇa. Simply try to love Him. That is perfection. That’s all. You cannot understand Kṛṣṇa. Nobody can understand Him. Kṛṣṇa Himself cannot understand Himself. Yes. \[*Laughter*\] He’s so *acintya*. And what to speak of us. Therefore our only business: how to love Kṛṣṇa, how to serve Kṛṣṇa. That’s all. That is perfection. You cannot understand Kṛṣṇa. Nobody can. Kṛṣṇa Himself cannot understand.

**Devotee:** The more we understand, the more we can convince others.

**Prabhupāda:** No. Better you understand this: that you cannot understand. This understanding is better.

**Devotee:** No, but what I said is that …

**Second devotee:** … that you have to give up. Just simply love Kṛṣṇa.

**Prabhupāda:** Yes. This is real understanding—that Kṛṣṇa cannot be understood. Simply let me love as far as possible, as I can, whatever is my … is in my capacity. That is perfection.

**Devotee:** Yes. But still we deal with this philosophy for …

**Prabhupāda:** Yes. On the whole, as Kṛṣṇa says, we can understand that. That’s all. You cannot understand Kṛṣṇa fully. It is not possible.

\* \* \*

Now all over the world the Aristotelian logic is dominating the lives of philosophers and scientists. These people judge the devotees of the Lord as being sentimentalists with no scientific understanding of the reality of this world. Meanwhile their lives are becoming more and more degraded under the pressure of their uncontrolled senses and mental frenzy.

The transcendental messenger of the Supreme Person takes upon himself the tremendous task of convincing these foolish souls to admit their inability to understand reality and to bring them to the point of surrender to *guru, sādhu,* and *śāstra*. In doing this he encounters so much obstinacy.

\* \* \*

**Prabhupāda:** Without knowing his capacity… He has no capacity, still he wants … And when he is caught—that you have no capacity—no, “I’m trying. I am trying.” He won’t say that “I have no capacity.” Therefore Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura said that “Whatever I heard from my *guru*—that is my life. That’s all. Let me execute that.”

\* \* \*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you brought to us the instructions of *guru, sādhu,* and *śāstra,* the illuminating reality of a life spent in executing the order of the spiritual master.

Your books are *guru, sādhu,* and *śāstra.* We should depend upon them for everything. There will be no confusion, no pride, no envy, no scarcity, and no quarrels if we simply dedicate our life to them. This can be done by reading, preaching, practicing, producing, printing, and distributing them.

Your movement is based on these books, which are the embodiment of your transcendental order. But Māyā’s plan is to introduce other ways of understanding, which are nothing but her orders in disguise.

Through your books and your transcendental projects like the Māyāpur Temple, the science of Godhead will shine brightly as the sole way out from the dark world of conditioning.

The ghosts of mental speculation and material attachment will run away in the presence of the effulgence of your devotion to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

We are very tiny—tiny intelligence, tiny strength, tiny devotion. We are not fit to fight Māyā. We need a devotional giant like yourself.

On this most auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray to Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi to let us always be protected from all sides by the instructions present in your books.

Our wish is to eternally celebrate the victory of our spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Śrī Śrīmād A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is the transcendental hero conquering over the darkness of illusion and pride, and to see his glories sung all over the three worlds.

Your servants at the Mediterranean division of the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust,  
(written by Madhusevita dāsa)

## North American BBT

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

It’s been fourteen years since the time you departed,  
Our service to you just barely got started.  
We thought you’d stay till 2000, we were caught off guard.  
It was the saddest day on earth.

I remember where I was when I heard the news.  
My mind was devastated as if it had blown a fuse.  
The one thing in my life I prayed I’d never lose—  
It was the saddest day of my life.

On the way back from the airport all I could do was cry.  
It was impossible to think that you would ever die.  
I had wasted so much time in “me and my.”  
It was the saddest day in the universe.

The service didn’t stop, not for a day.  
No one would dare change the way  
That we serve you, and try our debt to pay  
For the greatest opportunity after many, many births.

Spiritual life means that you’re still here,  
Although Kṛṣṇa wants you there because you’re so dear.  
Serving, praying to, and remembering makes me feel near.  
This day is the most wonderful of days every year.

You’re on the temple vyāsāsana, you’re there on the altar.  
You’re in our mind and consciousness whenever we don’t falter.  
ou’re in our heart, you’re in our soul, you’re always in our prayer.  
As Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee, you’re actually everywhere.

You’re the reason that we’re living, the reason we don’t mind death,  
The reason that we’re happy to be able to take another breath.  
We really don’t know Kṛṣṇa, we have a glimpse of knowing you.  
You’re in your books, in your tapes, you’re in your pictures, too.

Now we’ve all been still serving you, and it’s almost ‘92.  
Some have left, forgetting the debt of a lifetime of dues  
That we must pay for the privilege of having met you.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda please let us do something nice to help you push on your movement.

Your servants at the North American BBT,

## Swedish BBT

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

You said, “My Guru Mahārāja used to say that our *mṛdaṅga* can be heard for a few blocks’ distance, but our books are the great *mṛdaṅga* because they can be heard for thousands of miles. So I ask, Please continue to print and distribute the books, and in that way Kṛṣṇa consciousness will spread all over the world, and the people will wonder that this old man keeps beating his drum.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you beat the drum like no one else. Europe is drowning in an ocean of your books. Everyone is getting a chance to understand the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Everyone is getting your mercy.

People are writing to us from every nook and corner of this continent, praising your books, asking for more books, thanking you for changing their lives, thanking you for doing the greatest welfare work ever done.

Letters arrive from Siberian villages which cannot even be found on a map, letters arrive from towns beyond the polar circle, from big cities, from islands, and from deserts. Your books do wonders. The whole world is becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

One lady writes from Kiev: “I borrowed *Bhagavad-gītā Kak Ona Est,* by A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, from a friend, but I could not keep it for very long. Unable to bear the separation from the book, I made a handwritten copy. Now Kṛṣṇa is always here.”

A student writes from Vorkuta, Siberia: “In this city there is only one *Bhagavad-gītā.* I was number 58 on the waiting list. Please send another copy, because there are more than 200 people waiting who also want to read the book.”

One man writes from Warszawa: “I am 37, and I have been paralyzed for 36 years. In my life I only had ONE happy day—the day when I got the opportunity to read one of your books.”

As the material world falls apart, your books are the only reality that remains. Your books will never be devalued. Your books give permanent shelter in this transient mortal world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you very much for keeping us engaged in your ecstatic mission.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you very much for your most wonderful books.

Your humble servants at New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, Sweden (Swedish BBT),

## Homages from Other Sources

## Australian Food For Life

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you and your disciples.

You once said, Śrīla Prabhupāda, “This Hare Kṛṣṇa movement will be known as the kitchen religion.” From the mundane pont of view, this statement seems to minimize the authenticity of the ISKCON movement. However, all the devotees in your ISKCON society and all those people who have contacted ISKCON over the last twenty-five years will strongly testify to the value of this one simple statement.

In this Age of Kali, very, very few are interested in spiritual discipline. In fact, it is being seen that even our ISKCON family members are finding great difficulty in meeting the standards of *sādhana-bhakti.* What to speak of the general public!

In our temples around the world, we sometimes find very little attendance at *maṅgala-ārati* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, but as soon as *prasādam* is served, almost like magic devotees, guests, and strangers “come out of the woodwork” to honor the Lord’s mercy.

You knew, Śrīla Prabhupāda, right from the early days on Second Avenue how to win the hearts of the conditioned souls with this magical substance called *prasādam*. Like a wizard in the kitchen waving your spoon and chanting special *mantras* over the preparatoins, you captured the souls of your young followers.

Now, twenty-five years later, we are still experiencing the amazing power of Kṛṣṇa *prāsadam* to soften even the hard hearts here in Australia. Our Food for Life work has been going on steadily since 1978, and since that time we have received a lot of praise and credibility among the people here. Now in 1991, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are sincerely trying to push on this Food for Life mission in a big way. You always taught us to think big. “When shooting, you should shoot for the white rhinoceros, because if you succeed you will be glorified everywhere, and if you fail, the people will appreciate your sincere efforts, understanding that it was an impossible task anyway.”

Our plan, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to use this Food for Life work as a means to contact the most important people in the community and in this way gradually bring them to understand the other aspects of your teachings. Then hopefully, by their influence we will convince the masses of the benefits of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Already, we are having some small success in this regard—all due to your blessings. You loved to feed people Kṛṣṇa *prasādam,* and gradually we are also developing a “special taste” for doing the same.

Because Kṛṣṇa consciousness is synonymous with personalism, it is essential that as your followers we also try to imbibe the same mood of personalism you freely gave us all. By distributing Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* we are learning to deal with people as people. We become more sensitive and in tune with the problems they face.

Now in this present day, we see a total lack of care for others. Impersonalism is rampant. You came to deliver the Western countries from this impersonalism and voidism, and one of your secret weapons was this *prasādam* distribution.

Nothing you did, Śrīla Prabhupāda, was superficial. The simple and caring act of offering someone a plate of *prasādam* is essential, and not only that, but a very effective way of breaking down the barriers. It’s a perfect communications tool.

Therefore, on this very special day of your appearance anniversary, our sincere prayer is: “Please bless us to push on this Food for Life mission. Please protect us from *māyā’s* embrace, as we sometimes have to deal with very materialistic people in our promotion work. Please protect us from pride when we are sometimes successful. And most of all, please make us your instruments, for this is your mission, your desire, and your mercy upon us all.”

May that day come when “Hare Kṛṣṇa Food for Life” is a household name and your glories as the founder of this wonderful service are known to all.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your Food for Life servants in Australia,  
(written by Priyavrata dāsa)

## Back to Godhead Magazine

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet, which are giving shelter to all living entities. And all glories to your divine lotus magazine, *Back to Godhead,* which is bestowing the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu upon the misplaced refugees of Kali-yuga.

By your causeless mercy you have engaged us—your disciples, granddisciples, and aspiring granddisciples—in the transcendental activity of pushing forward *Back to Godhead* magazine even though we are quite unfit for this service. This is your causeless mercy upon us. Before you picked us up, we were sliding full speed ahead back to “doghead.”

With great struggle and difficulty you pushed forward BTG during its early days in India. Your printer in Delhi, Mr. Surendra Kumar Jain, saw that you were going through a great struggle just to get the magazine printed. He advised you to stop printing BTG. You told him, “No, Surendra Kumar, it is my mission. And one day you will see that I will succeed in my mission.”

In the early days of ISKCON here in America, you personally went and picked out the first printing press for BTG, an A. B. Dick mimeograph machine. During the many years you stayed with us, you put a tremendous amount of time and energy into seeing that the magazine was to the proper standard for pleasing your spiritual master and for attracting the victims of Kali-yuga to the relief ocean of Lord Caitanya’s mercy.

Now you have handed us the inconceivably illuminating torch of *Back to Godhead* magazine. You’ve asked us to keep it burning brightly and carry it to every town and village. Your desire is that your BTG ignite a revolution for the respiritualization of the entire human society. Please give us your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we can adequately rise to this occasion.

People are looking for relief from the suffering they are going through at every moment. They conjure up within their minds a sort of imaginary paradise in which they seek shelter and relief. But since there is no real substance to it, they are ultimately left with no protection. *Back to Godhead* is the soothing balm of your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, which will heal their troubled minds and guide them back to the real paradise, Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may be able to purely follow your teachings. Then when we ask people to subscribe to *Back to Godhead* they will be attracted by our personal example and will want to read BTG for the same peace of mind and happiness they see in us. Of course, on our own we have no such assets. But when we are properly situated in your service, we gradually develop all the saintly qualities.

Thank you for engaging all of us in your *Back to Godhead saṅkīrtana* party.

Your servants at Back to Godhead Magazine,  
(written by Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa)

Jayādvaita Swami / Mukunda Goswami / Umāpati Swami / Advaita Ācārya dāsa / Nāgarāja dāsa / Yamarāja dāsa / Arcita dāsa / Draviḍa dāsa / Drutakarmā dāsa / Navīna Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa / Śrī Rāma dāsa / Prāṇadā-devī dāsī / Viṣṇupriyā-devī dāsī / Manohāriṇī-devī dāsī

## The BBT Library Party

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

I was very inspired to read the instructions you wrote to Vaṁśīdhārī dāsa in 1970 regarding the distribution of your books in school libraries:

Bombay  
16th November, 1970

My Dear Bansidhari,  
I am very glad that your distribution to the libraries is well received. Please get our books at least one full set in every city and college library in your neighboring areas. And if you read these literatures carefully you will realize the topmost science of Krsna Consciousness and be more inspired to spread the same to all persons as their real welfare benefit.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are the devotee *Bhāgavata* who faithfully serves the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* it is not surprising to find that the same compassion you showed in your letter to Vaṁśīdhārī Prabhu is echoed in the statements of Krsna to the cowherd boys in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (10.22.35):

*etāvaj janma-sāphalyaṁ*  
*dehiṇām iha dehiṣu*  
*prāṇair arthair dhiyā vācā*  
*śreya-ācaraṇaṁ sadā*

**“It is the duty of every living being to perform welfare activities for the benefit of others with his life, wealth, intelligence, and words.”**

Our actual life in preaching means that we must always remain fixed in these instructions and exemplary in our behavior: *āpani ācari bhakti śikhāimu sabāre*. Our behavior must be exactly in accord with our preaching.

As a general is the source of inspiration and prowess in battle, so you are the general, Prabhupāda, in the midst of the Kali-yuga battleground, who protects us, your weak footsoldiers, from the terrible onslaught created by the fearsome weapons of *māyā’s* multifaceted vices. As stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.2.15),

*yad-anudhyāsinā yuktāḥ*  
*karma-granthi-nibandhanam*  
*chindanti kovidās tasya*  
*ko na kuryāt kathā-ratim*

**“With sword in hand, intelligent men cut through the binding knots of reactionary work (*****karma*****) by remembering the Personality of Godhead. Therefore, who will not pay attention to His message?”**

Yet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, without your ever-vigilant guidance, we can never hope to maintain our steadiness in your service. We pray to you for the determination to carefully read your transcendental books—your “emotional ecstasies”—which will always serve to maintain us throughout our lives, and which are the guiding light for all of humanity for the next ten thousand years.

ONLY by obtaining your compassionate glance will we receive the strength to place sets of your books in the numerous school libraries and in the homes of the suffering *jīvas,* as you desire.

Hoping to remain among your servants,  
The Bhaktivedanta Book Trust Library Party, India.

## Bhaktivedanta Gurukula Village

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustraraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsām*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

Our dear lord and master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this great and glorious occasion we are feeling the weight of our inability to understand or describe the unlimited qualities of Your Divine Grace. This is due to our attachment and unrestricted selfish desires to control and enjoy the material world and to falsely credit ourselves as the ultimate friend and well-wisher of all living entities. In this way we are blinded and overwhelmed, and with our stonelike hearts, dark and silent, we stand humbled before you, embarrassed and incompetent. We stand forlorn, gazing from a distance across the insurmountable ocean of nescience.

In you, though, we now see hope, in you we now see our salvation, rising from beyond this desolation, sent by the blessings of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Your every movement, your every word, an ecstatic boon—conquering the darkness that has engulfed us. This great mission of yours, the embodiment of the wishes of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, is giving us real life, is giving us real direction, re-educating us as to the actual values of this human form. We can now feel confident having been given the association of devotees and be assured of all success on our journey toward self-realization.

By your Bhaktivedanta purports, the essence of the sublime teachings of all the previous *ācāryas,* headed by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, has bloomed, making the true meaning available for all and saving us from the modern slaughterhouses of so-called education. Lord Caitanya, the perfect preacher of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* has sent you as the able captain of this ship of the message of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* for those who desire to cross the dangerous ocean of Kali.

We thus pray, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to become your worthy servants, to assist you in your eternal service to the previous *ācāryas,* Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava.

Begging to remain your eternal servants,  
The members of the Bhaktivedanta Gurukula Village, Māyāpur, India,

## Bhaktivedanta Archives

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Your arrival at Boston harbor was a momentous event that was unrecognized by the few observers present. Who could ever imagine that this elderly Indian person dressed in the simple cloth of a renunciant would spearhead a spiritual awakening in America and the world? But you did, and it was a struggle that seemed to offer nothing but defeat. Yet within a short time, the seed you planted in the hard, barren soil of materialistic New York sprouted. Although New York is such a big city, this tiny sprout caught everybody’s attention. It was scandalous! Hirsute hippies with beads around their necks were dancing with incense and flowers in Tompkins Square Park, chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa with the Swami. Soon these scraggly followers took your instructions seriously, chanting sixteen rounds of Hare Kṛṣṇa on beads and following the regulative principles. They shaved their heads and donned saffron robes. This shoot spread its limbs across America and throughout the world within a few short years. Without your ground-breaking work, the seed could not have sprouted; without the nurture you supplied, it could not have reached the global proportions it has.

It is said that the appearance of Kṛṣṇa in this world is always predicted in authorized *śāstras.* Similarly your appearance had been predicted by a previous *ācārya,* Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and your activities had been predicted by the Supreme Lord Himself, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra hoibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

It is clear to all that you have fulfilled the desire of Lord Caitanya, and it is our inestimable good fortune that we are your followers.

After performing the amazing feat of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, you exited the scene of material existence. This caused great lamentation and consternation for your disciples. To keep alive the memory of what you said, how you talked, how you dealt with people in different situations and what you looked like, the Bhaktivedanta Archives has been working steadily to preserve your published works, spoken instructions, photographs, and art. Indeed, this task has become our be-all and end-all, the sustaining force of our Kṛṣṇa conscious lives, and the powerful means for remaining always fixed in thoughts of you and your transcendental instructions.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Archives,

U.S.A.

Raṇajit dāsa, Ekanātha dāsa, Dulāla-candra dāsa, Prabhupādānanda dāsa / Kṣamā-devī dāsī

Europe

Viṣṇu-mūrti dāsa, Nāma-kṛṣṇa-devī dāsī, Bahulāśva dāsa, Candrāvalī-devī dāsī

## Bhaktivedanta School for Sanskrit Studies

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to your lotus feet and to the lotus feet of your disciples and granddisciples!

Fifteen years ago you put this thread around my neck and told me to now become a *brāhmaṇa*. Why, then, after all this time, am I still a miserly fool, ignoring the responsibility you so kindly offered:

*parīkṣya lokān karma-citān brāhmaṇo*  
*nirvedam āyān nāsty akṛtaḥ kṛtena*  
*tad-vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacchet*  
*samit-pāṇiḥ śrotriyaṁ brahma-niṣṭham*

**“One who is spiritually intelligent (a** ***brāhmaṇa*****) should see how all elevations to higher worlds are just material reactions to work. With this in mind, he should learn indifference, since the unmade eternal cannot be achieved by anything created. He should approach a spiritual master to obtain realized knowledge of this truth; carrying firewood in his hands, he should approach him who is steeped in the scriptures and dedicated to the Absolute” (*****Mundaka Upaniṣad*** **1.2.12).**

If someone is actually a *brāhmaṇa,* a person of broad intelligence, ready to try to understand the Supreme, he should learn first how to see the difference between reality and illusion. He should recognize the futility of his feverish striving for pleasure and prestige. And to do this he must get the mercy of a compassionate Vaiṣṇava, falling at his feet and begging for instruction and an opportunity to serve. To become a disciple in any real sense he must be eager to work as a humble assistant, to hear carefully and to diligently carry out the orders received. The Vaiṣṇava *sad-guru* deserves this surrender and is fully capable of reciprocating because his heart is enlightened with the perpetual truths of the revealed scriptures and because he has dedicated his whole existence to the Supreme Lord’s satisfaction.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the best example of such a spiritual master. You considered it your first business to make some Vaiṣṇava *brāhmaṇas,* even though, as you sometimes commented, turning Europeans and Americans into *brāhmaṇas* is like trying to wash coal. When this is accomplished, then the other half of your business in this world, re-instituting *varṇāśrama-dharma,* can actually begin. Few of us were born in the right kind of family or trained correctly for most of our lives. But by hearing and obeying your instructions, even we, and others, can come up to the standard. Please give us the courage, patience, and resolution to keep pushing on until we make the grade, each one of your followers—past, present and future.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta School for Sanskrit Studies,

Gopīparāṇadhana dāsa / Bīr Kṛṣṇa Goswami / Aja dāsa / Śyāmasundara dāsa / Jaya Balarāma dāsa / Rādhā-caraṇa dāsa / Romila Veish

## Bṛhat-mṛdaṅga Press

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Fourteen years have passed now since your divine departure from this temporary world of shadow and illusion. Devastating. This is the only word to describe the effect of your departure on your disciples, on ISKCON, on every living entity. You lit up the universe with your empowered presence and filled the heart of every living entity with the profound happiness and freedom felt only through dependence on Their eternal Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

Your perfect, divine purity, spiritual stamina, and unlimited devotion made the happiness we felt in your *vapuḥ* presence your own unique gift to us. It was a gift that cannot be equaled or replaced in this life by any other of the Lord’s servants. This we have—all of us hopefully by now—realized. You are—forever—special, unique, irreplaceable.

Time affects not only devastation but also reflection. Spiritual maturity should develop with physical and mental maturity. Maturity, i.e., the desire to listen to and follow more and more the Supersoul within, tells us that our perfection lies not in ludicrous imitation but in devoted following. Determined yet patient, bold yet humble. The reconciliation of opposites is possible only within mature spiritual life.

We must fix your divine instructions in our lives and aim the balance of our lives toward the attainment of that one glorious goal: that of bringing those oceanic 10,000-suns-bright smiles to your lotus face—again, again, and again. Your pleasure is Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pleasure is the source of our own eternal peace and happiness.

We thank you for the inconceivably fortunate opportunity to produce and print your *vāṇī—*transcendental literatures—for worldwide distribution. May we continue to experience your smile and be engaged eternally in determined devotional service wherever and however you desire.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at the Bṛhat-mṛdaṅga Press, Northampton, Massachusetts,

## Hawaii (Big Island)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this glorious day of your appearance.

Thank you for so many uncountable blessings, and especially for your translation and commentary on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* which is the unadulterated, all-blissful ripened fruit of the tree of Vedic knowledge. Even more, thank you for your translation and commentary on *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* which is the only solace for one bereft of all shelter within this material world. Even more, thank you for your wonderful pastimes, which mean so much more than anything else to us who are so far fallen from Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s association. Meditation on your pastimes quenches the thirst of a heart parched in this desert of bad association.

Thank you for making the process of Deity worship possible for us by stressing punctuality, cleanliness, and, above all, love and devotion, and not overwhelming us with complex rituals.

Thank you for your temples in Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur, which have given us a shelter in these transcendental abodes.

Thank you for the association of our Godbrothers and -sisters, whereby we may become strong and give up unwanted material desires.

Thank you for continuing to appear in our dreams, which need you in the center to avoid becoming nightmares.

Thank you for *prasādam,* which is something simply wonderful.

Thank you for the mood of detachment from the material world, by which one can be inoculated against all its pinching miseries.

Above all, thank you for being the bona fide representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and thus revealing to us the myriad rainbow of ever-expanding good qualities and mercy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who had long been forgotten or neglected throughout the world.

And finally, thank you for having been the all-time perfect example of a devotee so that those of us who have had your direct or indirect association may never be cheated by those who try to imitate you or diminish your importance, but who are in actual fact nothing but the shadow of its potency.

Your humble disciples and granddisciples on the big island of Hawaii, still inadequately trying to become your servants,

## ISKCON Television (ITV)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your everlasting service!

What’s the Point?

What’s the point …  
…if we work hard all day  
…just for food and a little pay?

What’s the point …  
…if we beget children for fun  
…and they do what we have done?

What’s the point …  
…if we live a life of charity  
…while doing no spiritual austerity?

What’s the point …  
…if we listen to sound  
…where nothing transcendental is found?

What’s the point …  
…if sleep rests our head  
…but our soul remains so dead?

What’s the point …  
…if the thoughts in our mind  
…dwell only in ideas of “me” and “mine”?

What’s the point …  
…if we follow a leader  
…who we later find is only a cheater?

What’s the point …  
…if we keep our body healthy and fit  
…only to ruthlessly abuse it?

What’s the point …  
if we write sweet words  
offered to undeserving lords?

What’s the point …  
if we become so smart  
but ignore Govinda in the heart?

What’s the point …  
if we are messing around  
in the material world, going round and round?

What’s the point …  
if we don’t honor you,  
who offer love and devotion too?

The point is …  
we are spirit souls,  
we don’t get rich and we don’t get old.  
We are far from home in the spiritual world,  
but you bring us out of this awful mold.  
“Chant the holy name!” you cry.  
Our deaf ears can hear only “I.”  
Yet through the darkness  
your voice penetrates  
with patience, knowledge, and a kind embrace.

*rāmādi-mūrtiṣu kalā-niyamena tiṣṭhan*  
*nānāvatāram akarod bhuvaneṣu kintu*  
*kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi*

**“I worship the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Govinda, who is always situated in various incarnations such as Rāma and Nṛsiṁha, and many subincarnations as well, but who is the original Personality of Godhead known as Kṛṣṇa and who incarnates personally also” (*****Brahma-saṁhita*** **5.39).**

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You saved us from the onslaughts of each day. The process you have given us is the ultimate twelve-step program, the best seminar, the most valuable encounter group, the definitive *guru*-trip, the finest advanced degree course, the last word in outward-bound excursions, the goal of all *yoga,* and the most delicious path back to Godhead. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, from all of us at ITV and the Pyramid Center for Bhakti Yoga. That’s the point.

Your servants at ITV,

Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa / Siddhānta dāsa / Śacīdulāl-devī dāsī / Jīvānanda dāsa / Īśāna dāsa / Girirāja dāsa / Mohana-devī dāsī / Ajita-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Lisa

## ISKCON World Review

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We here at *ISKCON World Review* feel awed and inspired by the privileged vantage point you have given us. Your devotional service allows us to witness the glory of your developing society—devotees cooperating all over the world to advance the culture of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

This promontory, which has been given to us purely by your mercy, is perhaps one of the best places to appreciate the many manifestations of your planetary preaching. What we get to see is how your followers, disciples, well-wishers, and supporters contribute to the spreading of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness. And they are doing it by the best means possible—courageously and cooperatively serving you together, according to your instructions.

And even more than that, you kindly allow us to print and distribute these stories so that others, too, may be further inspired by you and connected to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we see so many wonderful changes in the world. Devotees are now free to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness behind what used to be called the Iron Curtain. To the uninformed onlooker, these things appear to be happening as a result of political circumstances. But we are convinced that it is the widespread chanting of the holy name, which bears all of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s potency, and massive distribution of your books that are causing these effects.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that we always remain at your lotus feet. Please allow us to continue to serve your society. Teach us to be good communicators and cooperators. Please help us expand your transcendental newspaper. Help us broadcast the beating of Lord Caitanya’s *bṛhat mṛdaṅga* further and further, louder and louder!

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON World Review.

## KrishnaFest

To His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājak-ācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta svāmin iti nāmine*

*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dear beloved Guru Mahārāja,

Proud of our sins and fins and teeth,  
Feeding on each other, bitten by grief,  
We, lowly sealife, captured and rescued,  
You dragged us out of the vast bhava-sindhu.

With Madana-mohana’s beauty, so bright,  
You lured us through our sense of sight.  
Your prasādam bait caught our taste  
And put our equilibrium to waste.

Your ISKCON troller plied far and near  
Through troubled waters, devoid of fear,  
Suddenly our minds were snagged by your hooks,  
Those irresistibly sharpened books.

Unlike an ordinary fisherman,  
You tossed us out to sea again—  
The sportive ocean of saṅkīrtana,  
Ānandāmbudhi-vardhanam.

Now kindly hear this aquatic’s plea:  
Concerned we are for those rotting at sea;  
To bring them home is our ardent wish;  
Let us educate the schools of fish.

Your servants at KrishnaFest,
